## Proceeds of Crime (Money Laundering) and Terrorist Financing Regulations {#proceeds-of-crime-money-laundering-and-terrorist-financing-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2002-184
:::

[PROCEEDS OF CRIME (MONEY LAUNDERING) AND TERRORIST FINANCING
ACT](/eng/acts/P-24.501)

Registration 2002-05-09

Proceeds of Crime (Money Laundering) and Terrorist Financing Regulations

P.C. 2002-781 2002-05-09
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the recommendation of
the Minister of Finance, pursuant to subsection 73(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e){#footnotea_e-ID0EGCAA} of the
[Proceeds of Crime (Money Laundering) and Terrorist Financing
Act](/eng/acts/P-24.501)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#footnoteb_e){#footnoteb_e-ID0EDCAA}, hereby makes
the annexed [Proceeds of Crime (Money Laundering) and Terrorist
Financing Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2002-184).

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#footnotea_e-ID0EGCAA){#footnotea_e}S.C. 2001, c.
  41, s. 73
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#footnoteb_e-ID0EDCAA){#footnoteb_e}S.C. 2000, c.
  17; 2001, c. 41, s. 48
  :::
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-682541 .Part}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2007-293, s. 5(F)

\]
:::

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In the
  Act and in these Regulations, [[shell bank]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
  a foreign financial institution that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} does not have a place of business that

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is located at a fixed address --- where it
      employs one or more persons on a full-time basis and maintains
      operating records related to its banking activities --- in a
      country in which it is authorized to conduct banking activities,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is subject to inspection by the regulatory
      authority that licensed it to conduct banking activities; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not controlled by, or under common control with,
    a depository institution, credit union or foreign financial
    institution that maintains a place of business referred to in
    paragraph (a) in Canada or in a foreign country.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[accountant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[accountant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a chartered accountant, a
      certified general accountant, a certified management accountant
      or, if applicable, a chartered professional
      accountant. ([comptable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[accounting firm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[accounting firm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity that is
      engaged in the business of providing accounting services to the
      public and has at least one partner, employee or administrator
      that is an accountant. ([cabinet d'expertise
      comptable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Proceeds of Crime (Money
      Laundering) and Terrorist Financing
      Act](/eng/acts/P-24.501). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[annuity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[annuity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([rente]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[authorized user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[authorized user]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who is
      authorized by a holder of a prepaid payment product account to
      have electronic access to funds or virtual currency available in
      the account by means of a prepaid payment product that is
      connected to it. ([utilisateur autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[British Columbia notary corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[British Columbia notary corporation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      an entity that carries on the business of providing notary
      services to the public in British Columbia in accordance with the
      Notaries Act, R.S.B.C. 1996, c. 334. ([société de notaires de la
      Colombie-Britannique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[British Columbia notary public]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[British Columbia notary public]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      person who is a member of the Society of Notaries Public of
      British Columbia. ([notaire public de la
      Colombie-Britannique]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[business relationship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[business relationship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2019-240, s. 22\]]{.Repealed}

  [[cash]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[cash]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means coins referred to in section 7
      of the [Currency Act](/eng/acts/C-52), notes issued by the Bank of
      Canada under the [Bank of Canada Act](/eng/acts/B-2) that are
      intended for circulation in Canada or coins or bank notes of
      countries other than Canada. ([espèces]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[casino]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[casino]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a government, organization,
      board or operator that is referred to in any of paragraphs 5(k) to
      (k.3) of the Act. ([casino]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CICA Handbook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CICA Handbook]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed, SOR/2019-240, s.
      22\]]{.Repealed}

  [[client credit file]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[client credit file]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2016-153, s. 15\]]{.Repealed}

  [[client information record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[client information record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2019-240, s. 22\]]{.Repealed}

  [[correspondent banking relationship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[correspondent banking relationship]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as in subsection 9.4(3) of the Act. ([relation de
      correspondant bancaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[credit union central]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[credit union central]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a central
      cooperative credit society, as defined in section 2 of the
      [Cooperative Credit Associations Act](/eng/acts/C-41.01), or a
      credit union central or a federation of credit unions or caisses
      populaires that is regulated by a provincial Act other than one
      enacted by the legislature of Quebec. ([centrale de caisses de
      crédit]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[crowdfunding platform]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[crowdfunding platform]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a website or
      an application or other software that is used to raise funds or
      virtual currency through donations. ([plateforme de
      sociofinancement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[crowdfunding platform services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[crowdfunding platform services]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      provision and maintenance of a crowdfunding platform for use by
      other persons or entities to raise funds or virtual currency for
      themselves or for persons or entities specified by
      them. ([services de plateforme de
      sociofinancement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[dealer in precious metals and precious stones]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[dealer in precious metals and precious
      stones]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or entity that, in the
      course of their business activities, buys or sells precious
      metals, precious stones or jewellery. It includes a department or
      an agent of His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or
      mandatary of His Majesty in right of a province when the
      department or the agent or mandatary carries out the activity,
      referred to in subsection 65(1), of selling precious metals to the
      public. ([négociant en métaux précieux et pierres
      précieuses]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[deferred profit sharing plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deferred profit sharing plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([régime de participation différée aux
      bénéfices]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[deposit slip]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deposit slip]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a record that sets out

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of the deposit;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person or entity that makes the
        deposit;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the deposit and of any part of it
        that is made in cash;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the method by which the deposit is made; and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the number of the account into which the
        deposit is made and the name of each account holder. ([relevé de
        dépôt]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[electronic funds transfer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[electronic funds transfer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      transmission --- by any electronic, magnetic or optical means ---
      of instructions for the transfer of funds, including a
      transmission of instructions that is initiated and finally
      received by the same person or entity. In the case of SWIFT
      messages, only SWIFT MT-103 messages and their equivalent are
      included. It does not include a transmission of instructions for
      the transfer of funds

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} [\[Repealed, SOR/2022-76, s. 1\]]{.Repealed}

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} that involves the beneficiary withdrawing cash
        from their account;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that is carried out by means of a direct
        deposit or a pre-authorized debit;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that is carried out by cheque imaging and
        presentment;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} that is both initiated and finally received by
        persons or entities that are acting to clear or settle payment
        obligations between themselves; or

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} that is initiated or finally received by a
        person or entity referred to in paragraphs 5(a) to (h.1) of the
        Act for the purpose of internal treasury management, including
        the management of their financial assets and liabilities, if one
        of the parties to the transaction is a subsidiary of the other
        or if they are subsidiaries of the same
        corporation. ([télévirement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[employees profit sharing plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[employees profit sharing plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([régime de participation des employés aux
      bénéfices]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[factor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or entity that is
      engaged in the business of factoring, with or without recourse
      against the assignor. ([affactureur]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[fiat currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fiat currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a currency that is
      issued by a country and is designated as legal tender in that
      country. ([monnaie fiduciaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[final receipt]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[final receipt]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an electronic
      funds transfer, means the receipt of the instructions by the
      person or entity that is to make the remittance to a
      beneficiary. ([destinataire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[financial entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[financial entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is referred to in any of
        paragraphs 5(a), (b) and (d) to (f) of the Act;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a financial services cooperative;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a life insurance company, or an entity that is
        a life insurance broker or agent, in respect of loans or prepaid
        payment products that it offers to the public and accounts that
        it maintains with respect to those loans or prepaid payment
        products, other than

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} loans that are made by the insurer to a
          policy holder if the insured person has a terminal illness
          that significantly reduces their life expectancy and the loan
          is secured by the value of an insurance policy;

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} loans that are made by the insurer to the
          policy holder for the sole purpose of funding the life
          insurance policy; and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} advance payments to which the policy holder
          is entitled that are made to them by the insurer;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a credit union central when it offers financial
        services to a person, or to an entity that is not a member of
        that credit union central; and

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a department, or an entity that is an agent of
        His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His
        Majesty in right of a province, when it carries out an activity
        referred to in section 76. ([entité financière]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[financial services cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[financial services cooperative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      financial services cooperative that is regulated by An Act
      respecting financial services cooperatives, CQLR, c. C-67.3, other
      than a caisse populaire. ([coopérative de services
      financiers]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[financing or leasing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[financing or leasing entity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
      or entity that is engaged in the business of financing or
      leasing. ([entité de financement ou de bail]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[foreign currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a fiat currency
      that is issued by a country other than
      Canada. ([devise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign currency exchange transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign currency exchange
      transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an exchange, at the
      request of another person or entity, of one fiat currency for
      another. ([opération de change en devise]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[foreign currency exchange transaction ticket]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign currency exchange transaction
      ticket]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a record respecting a foreign
      currency exchange transaction --- including an entry in a
      transaction register --- that sets out

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of the transaction;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a transaction of \$3,000 or
        more, the name and address of the person or entity that requests
        the exchange, the nature of their principal business or their
        occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each of the fiat
        currencies involved in the payment made and received by the
        person or entity that requests the exchange;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the method by which the payment is made and
        received;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
        the transaction, the type of account and the name of each
        account holder; and

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
        transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
        number. ([fiche d'opération de change en
        devise]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign money services business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign money services business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      person or entity referred to in paragraph 5(h.1) of the
      Act. ([entreprise de services monétaires
      étrangère]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[funds]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[funds]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cash and other fiat currencies, and securities,
        negotiable instruments or other financial instruments that
        indicate a title or right to or interest in them; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a private key of a cryptographic system that
        enables a person or entity to have access to a fiat currency
        other than cash.

      For greater certainty, it does not include virtual
      currency. ([fonds]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[information record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[information record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a record that
      sets out the name and address of a person or entity and

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a person, their date of birth
        and the nature of their principal business or their occupation;
        and

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity, the nature of its
        principal business. ([dossier de
        renseignements]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[initiation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[initiation]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an electronic
      funds transfer, means the first transmission of the instructions
      for the transfer of funds. ([amorcer]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[international electronic funds transfer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[international electronic funds
      transfer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an electronic funds transfer
      other than for the transfer of funds within Canada. ([télévirement
      international]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[[inter vivos]{.otherLang lang="la"} trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[[inter vivos]{.otherLang lang="la"}
      trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a personal trust, other than a
      trust created by will. ([fiducie entre vifs]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[jewellery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[jewellery]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means objects that are made of
      gold, silver, palladium, platinum, pearls or precious stones and
      that are intended to be worn as a personal
      adornment. ([bijou]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[large cash transaction record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[large cash transaction record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      record that indicates the receipt of an amount of \$10,000 or more
      in cash in a single transaction and that contains the following
      information:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of the receipt;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the amount is received for deposit into an
        account, the number of the account, the name of each account
        holder and the time of the deposit or an indication that the
        deposit is made in a night deposit box outside the recipient's
        normal business hours;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of every other person or
        entity that is involved in the transaction, the nature of their
        principal business or their occupation and, in the case of a
        person, their date of birth;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each fiat currency
        involved in the receipt;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the method by which the cash is received;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the exchange rates used and
        their source;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the number of every other account that is
        affected by the transaction, the type of account and the name of
        each account holder;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
        transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
        number;

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the purpose of the transaction;

      - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the following details of the remittance of, or
        in exchange for, the cash received:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the method of remittance,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is in funds, the type and
          amount of each type of funds involved,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is not in funds, the type
          of remittance and its value, if different from the amount of
          cash received, and

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name of every person or entity involved
          in the remittance and their account number or policy number
          or, if they have no account number or policy number, their
          identifying number; and

      - [(k)]{.lawlabel} if the amount is received by a dealer in
        precious metals and precious stones for the sale of precious
        metals, precious stones or jewellery,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the type of precious metals, precious stones
          or jewellery,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the value of the precious metals, precious
          stones or jewellery, if different from the amount of cash
          received, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the wholesale value of the precious metals,
          precious stones or jewellery. ([relevé d'opération importante
          en espèces]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[large virtual currency transaction record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[large virtual currency transaction
      record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a record that indicates the
      receipt of an amount of \$10,000 or more in virtual currency in a
      single transaction and that contains the following information:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of the receipt;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the amount is received for deposit into an
        account, the name of each account holder;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of every other person or
        entity that is involved in the transaction, the nature of their
        principal business or their occupation and, in the case of a
        person, their date of birth;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each virtual currency
        involved in the receipt;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the number of every other account that is
        affected by the transaction, the type of account and the name of
        each account holder;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
        transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
        number;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} every transaction identifier, including the
        sending and receiving addresses; and

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the amount is received by a dealer in
        precious metals and precious stones for the sale of precious
        metals, precious stones or jewellery,

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the type of precious metals, precious stones
          or jewellery,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the value of the precious metals, precious
          stones or jewellery, if different from the amount of virtual
          currency received, and

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the wholesale value of the precious metals,
          precious stones or jewellery. ([relevé d'opération importante
          en monnaie virtuelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[legal firm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[legal firm]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an entity that is engaged
      in the business of providing legal services to the
      public. ([cabinet d'avocats]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[life insurance broker or agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[life insurance broker or agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      person or entity that is authorized under provincial legislation
      to carry on the business of arranging contracts of life
      insurance. ([représentant d'assurance-vie]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[life insurance company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[life insurance company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a life
      company or foreign life company to which the [Insurance Companies
      Act](/eng/acts/I-11.8) applies or a life insurance company
      regulated by a provincial Act. ([société
      d'assurance-vie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[money services business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[money services business]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or
      entity referred to in paragraph 5(h) of the Act. ([entreprise de
      services monétaires]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mortgage administrator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mortgage administrator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or
      entity, other than a financial entity, that is engaged in the
      business of servicing mortgage agreements on real property or
      hypothec agreements on immovables on behalf of a
      lender. ([administrateur hypothécaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[mortgage broker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mortgage broker]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or entity
      that is authorized under provincial legislation to act as an
      intermediary between a lender and a borrower with respect to loans
      secured by mortgages on real property or hypothecs on
      immovables. ([courtier hypothécaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[mortgage lender]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[mortgage lender]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or entity,
      other than a financial entity, that is engaged in the business of
      providing loans secured by mortgages on real property or hypothecs
      on immovables. ([prêteur hypothécaire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ongoing monitoring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ongoing monitoring]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2019-240, s. 22\]]{.Repealed}

  [[passenger vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[passenger vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a motor vehicle
      --- other than an ambulance, a hearse, a motor vehicle that is
      clearly marked for policing activities, a motor vehicle that is
      clearly marked and equipped for emergency medical response
      activities or emergency fire response activities or a utility
      truck --- that is designed or adapted primarily to carry no more
      than 10 individuals on highways and streets. ([véhicule de
      tourisme]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[physical presence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[physical presence]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2019-240, s. 22\]]{.Repealed}

  [[precious metal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[precious metal]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means gold, silver,
      palladium or platinum in the form of coins, bars, ingots or
      granules or in any other similar form. ([métal
      précieux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[precious stones]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[precious stones]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means diamonds, sapphires,
      emeralds, tanzanite, rubies or alexandrite. ([pierre
      précieuse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prepaid payment product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prepaid payment product]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a product
      that is issued by a financial entity and that enables a person or
      entity to engage in a transaction by giving them electronic access
      to funds or virtual currency paid to a prepaid payment product
      account held with the financial entity in advance of the
      transaction. It excludes a product that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} enables a person or entity to access a credit
        or debit account or one that is issued for use only with
        particular merchants; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is issued for single use for the purposes of a
        retail rebate program. ([produit de paiement
        prépayé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[prepaid payment product account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[prepaid payment product account]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an
      account --- other than an account to which only a public body or,
      if doing so for the purposes of humanitarian aid, a registered
      charity as defined in subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3), can add funds or virtual currency --- that
      is connected to a prepaid payment product and that permits

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} funds or virtual currency that total \$1,000 or
        more to be added to the account within a 24-hour period; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a balance of funds or virtual currency of
        \$1,000 or more to be maintained. ([compte de produit de
        paiement prépayé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[public body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[public body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a department or an agent of His Majesty in
        right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His Majesty in right
        of a province;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an incorporated city or town, village,
        metropolitan authority, township, district, county, rural
        municipality or other incorporated municipal body in Canada or
        an agent or mandatary in Canada of any of them; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an organization that operates a public hospital
        and that is designated by the Minister of National Revenue as a
        hospital authority under the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15),
        or an agent or mandatary of such an organization. ([organisme
        public]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[real estate broker or sales representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[real estate broker or sales
      representative]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or entity that
      is authorized under provincial legislation to act as an agent or
      mandatary for purchasers or vendors in respect of the purchase or
      sale of real property or immovables. ([courtier ou agent
      immobilier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[real estate developer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[real estate developer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or
      entity that, in any calendar year after 2007, has sold to the
      public, other than in the capacity of a real estate broker or
      sales representative,

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} five or more new houses or condominium units;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} one or more new commercial or industrial
        buildings; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} one or more new multi-unit residential
        buildings each of which contains five or more residential units,
        or two or more new multi-unit residential buildings that
        together contain five or more residential units. ([promoteur
        immobilier]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[receipt of funds record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[receipt of funds record]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a record
      that indicates the receipt of an amount of funds and that contains
      the following information:

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of the receipt;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the amount is received from a person, their
        name, address and date of birth and the nature of their
        principal business or their occupation;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the amount is received from or on behalf of
        an entity, the entity's name and address and the nature of its
        principal business;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the funds received and of any
        part of the funds that is received in cash;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the method by which the amount is received;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each fiat currency
        involved in the receipt;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if applicable, the exchange rates used and
        their source;

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
        the transaction in which the receipt occurs, the type of account
        and the name of each account holder;

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of every other person or
        entity that is involved in the transaction, the nature of their
        principal business or their occupation and, in the case of a
        person, their date of birth;

      - [(j)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
        transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
        number; and

      - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the purpose of the transaction. ([relevé de
        réception de fonds]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[registered pension plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registered pension plan]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same
      meaning as in subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([régime de pension agréé]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[registered retirement income fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registered retirement income fund]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the
      same meaning as in subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax
      Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3). ([fonds enregistré de revenu de
      retraite]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[securities dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[securities dealer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person or entity
      that is referred to in paragraph 5(g) of the Act. ([courtier en
      valeurs mobilières]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[senior officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[senior officer]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an entity,
      means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a director of the entity who is one of its
        full-time employees;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the entity's chief executive officer, chief
        operating officer, president, secretary, treasurer, controller,
        chief financial officer, chief accountant, chief auditor or
        chief actuary, or any person who performs any of those
        functions; or

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} any other officer who reports directly to the
        entity's board of directors, chief executive officer or chief
        operating officer. ([cadre dirigeant]{.DefinedTermLink
        lang="fr"})

  [[signature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[signature]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes an electronic signature
      or other information in electronic form that is created or adopted
      by a client of a person or entity referred to in section 5 of the
      Act and that is accepted by the person or entity as being unique
      to that client. ([signature]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[signature card]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[signature card]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in respect of an account,
      means a document that is signed by a person who is authorized to
      give instructions in respect of the account, or electronic data
      that constitutes the signature of such a
      person. ([fiche-signature]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[SWIFT]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[SWIFT]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Society for Worldwide
      Interbank Financial Telecommunication. ([SWIFT]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[transaction ticket]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[transaction ticket]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}[\[Repealed,
      SOR/2019-240, s. 22\]]{.Repealed}

  [[trust company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[trust company]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a company that is
      referred to in any of paragraphs 5(d) to (e.1) of the
      Act. ([société de fiducie]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[virtual currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[virtual currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a digital representation of value that can be
        used for payment or investment purposes, that is not a fiat
        currency and that can be readily exchanged for funds or for
        another virtual currency that can be readily exchanged for
        funds; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a private key of a cryptographic system that
        enables a person or entity to have access to a digital
        representation of value referred to in paragraph (a). ([monnaie
        virtuelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[virtual currency exchange transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[virtual currency exchange
      transaction]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an exchange, at the
      request of another person or entity, of virtual currency for
      funds, funds for virtual currency or one virtual currency for
      another. ([opération de change en monnaie
      virtuelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[virtual currency exchange transaction ticket]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[virtual currency exchange transaction
      ticket]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a record respecting a virtual
      currency exchange transaction --- including an entry in a
      transaction register --- that sets out

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of the transaction;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a transaction of \$1,000 or
        more, the name and address of the person or entity that requests
        the exchange, the nature of their principal business or their
        occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds and
        each of the virtual currencies involved in the payment made and
        received by the person or entity that requests the exchange;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the method by which the payment is made and
        received;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
        the transaction, the type of account and the name of each
        account holder;

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
        transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
        number; and

      - [(h)]{.lawlabel} every transaction identifier, including the
        sending and receiving addresses. ([fiche d'opération de change
        en monnaie virtuelle]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of subsection 9.3(1) of the Act, a prescribed family member
  of a politically exposed foreign person, a politically exposed
  domestic person or a head of an international organization is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} their spouse or common-law partner;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} their child;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} their mother or father;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the mother or father of their spouse or common-law
    partner; or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a child of their mother or father.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of the definitions [[head of an
  international organization]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} and [[politically
  exposed domestic person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 9.3(3)
  of the Act, the prescribed period is five years.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-153, s. 18(E)
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 23](#1179427-1293983){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179427-1293983.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-2-20160630.html "Link to previous version of section 2"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
paragraph 5(l) of the Act, the prescribed precious metals are those
defined as [[precious metals]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} in subsection 1(2).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-122, s. 21
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 23](#1179427-1293986){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179427-1293986.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-3-20080623.html "Link to previous version of section 3"){.PITLink}

**[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of these
Regulations, an entity is affiliated with another entity if one of them
is wholly owned by the other, if both are wholly owned by the same
entity or if their financial statements are consolidated.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 23](#1179427-1293990){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179427-1293990.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-4-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 4"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[4.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
these Regulations, a person or entity to which section 5 of the Act
applies enters into a business relationship with a client at the
earliest of the following events:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the time when the person or entity opens an account
  for the client, except in the circumstances set out in any of
  paragraphs 154(1)(a) to (d) and (2)(a) to (l) and (p) and subsection
  154(3),

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the second time that the person or entity is required
  to verify the identity of the client under these Regulations,

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the person or entity is a real estate broker or
  sales representative, real estate developer, mortgage administrator,
  mortgage broker or mortgage lender, the first time that the person or
  entity is required to verify the identity of the client under these
  Regulations,

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the person or entity is a money services business
  and the client is an entity, the time when the person or entity enters
  into a service agreement with the client to provide a service referred
  to in any of subparagraphs 5(h)(i) to (v) of the Act,

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the person or entity is a foreign money services
  business and the client is an entity in Canada, the time when the
  person or entity enters into a service agreement with the client to
  provide a service referred to in any of subparagraphs 5(h.1)(i) to (v)
  of the Act, and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the person or entity is a financing or leasing
  entity, the time when the person or entity enters into a financing or
  leasing arrangement with the client in connection with an activity
  referred to in section 24.15.

## [PART 1]{.HLabel1}[Requirements To Report Transactions and To Keep Records]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1294000 .Part}

### [Application of Parts 5 and 6]{.HTitleText2} {#h-682681 .Subheading}

**[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** The provisions of this Part
are subject to the provisions of Parts 5 and 6.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2008-21, s. 4
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 23](#1179427-1294003){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179427-1294003.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-5-20090928.html "Link to previous version of section 5"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Financial Entities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-682687 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2016-153, s. 19(E)

\]
:::

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  financial services cooperative is engaged in a business or profession
  for the purposes of paragraph 5(i) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A credit union central is engaged in a business or
  profession for the purposes of paragraph 5(j) of the Act when it
  offers financial services to a person, or to an entity that is not a
  member of that credit union central.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-153, s. 20(E)
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 23](#1179427-1294010){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179427-1294010.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-6-20160630.html "Link to previous version of section 6"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  financial entity shall report the following transactions and
  information to the Centre:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the receipt from a person or entity of an amount of
    \$10,000 or more in cash in a single transaction, together with the
    information set out in Schedule 1;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the initiation, at the request of a person or
    entity, of an international electronic funds transfer of \$10,000 or
    more in a single transaction, together with the information set out
    in Schedule 2;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the final receipt of an international electronic
    funds transfer of \$10,000 or more in a single transaction, together
    with the information set out in Schedule 3; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the receipt from a person or entity of an amount of
    \$10,000 or more in virtual currency in a single transaction,
    together with the information set out in Schedule 4.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A financial entity is not required to report the
  transaction and information under paragraph (1)(a), if the amount is
  received from another financial entity or a public body or from a
  person who is acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity
  or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-122, s. 22
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 23](#1179427-1294018){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179427-1294018.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-7-20080623.html "Link to previous version of section 7"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  financial entity is not required to report the transaction and
  information under paragraph 7(1)(a) if the following conditions are
  met:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the entity from which or on whose behalf the
    financial entity receives the amount is a corporation that carries
    on business as an establishment described in sector 22, 44
    (excluding codes 4411, 4412 and 44831) or 45 (excluding code 45392),
    or code 481, 482, 485 (excluding code 4853), 51711, 51751, 61121 or
    61131 of the North American Industry Classification System as that
    sector or code read on January 31, 2003;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the amount received is deposited into an account
    held by the corporation with the financial entity in respect of that
    business;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the corporation has had

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for the entire preceding 24-month period, an
      account in respect of that business with the financial entity, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an account in respect of that business with
      another financial entity for a continuous period of 24 months
      ending immediately before the corporation opened an account with
      the financial entity;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the financial entity has records that indicate that
    the corporation has deposited an amount of \$10,000 or more in cash
    into that account on an average of at least twice a week during the
    preceding 12 months;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the cash deposits made by the corporation are
    consistent with the corporation's usual practice in respect of the
    business;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the financial entity has taken reasonable measures
    to determine the source of the cash for those deposits; and

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the financial entity provides to the Centre the
    information set out in Schedule 5.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A corporation referred to in paragraph (1)(a) does
  not include one that carries on a business related to pawnbroking or
  whose principal business is the sale of vehicles, vessels, farm
  machinery, aircraft, mobile homes, jewellery, precious stones,
  precious metals, antiquities or art.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A financial entity that, in accordance with
  subsection (1), does not report a transaction and information

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall report to the Centre a change to the
    corporation's name or address, the nature of the corporation's
    business or the corporation's incorporation number within 15 days
    after the day on which the change is made; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall, at least once every 12 months,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} verify that the conditions referred to in
      subsection (1) continue to be met,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} ensure that a senior officer of the financial
      entity confirms that those conditions continue to be met, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} send a report to the Centre setting out the
      corporation's name and address and the name of the senior officer
      who gave the confirmation.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-122, s. 76
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 23](#1179427-1294038){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179427-1294038.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-8-20080623.html "Link to previous version of section 8"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** A financial entity that
maintains a list of clients for the purposes of subsection 9(3) of the
Act shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} include the name and address of each client in the
  list; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} maintain the list in paper form, or in a
  machine-readable or electronic form if a paper copy can readily be
  produced from it.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 25](#1179433-1294484){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179433-1294484.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-9-20080623.html "Link to previous version of section 9"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** A financial entity shall
keep a large cash transaction record in respect of every amount of
\$10,000 or more in cash that it receives from a person or entity in a
single transaction, unless the amount is received from another financial
entity or a public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a
client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-122, s. 76
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 25](#1179433-1294067){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179433-1294067.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-10-20080623.html "Link to previous version of section 10"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** A financial entity shall
keep a large virtual currency transaction record in respect of every
amount of \$10,000 or more in virtual currency that it receives from a
person or entity in a single transaction, unless the amount is received
from another financial entity or a public body or from a person who is
acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-293, s. 8
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 25](#1179433-1294070){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179433-1294070.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-11-20080623.html "Link to previous version of section 11"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** A financial entity shall
keep the following records in respect of every account that it opens and
of every transaction that is conducted with it, other than those
referred to in section 13 or 14:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} every signature card;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a record for each account holder and every other
  person --- up to three, in the case of a business account --- who is
  authorized to give instructions in respect of the account, containing
  their name and address, the nature of their principal business or
  their occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if an account holder is a corporation, a copy of the
  part of its official corporate records that contains any provision
  relating to the power to bind the corporation in respect of the
  account or transaction;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a record that sets out the intended use of the
  account;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a record of every application in respect of the
  account;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} every account operating agreement that it creates or
  receives in respect of the account;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} a deposit slip in respect of every deposit that is
  made into the account;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} every debit and credit memo that it creates or
  receives in respect of the account, except debit memos that relate to
  another account at the same branch of the financial entity that
  created the debit memo;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} a copy of every account statement that it sends to an
  account holder;

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} every cleared cheque that is drawn on, and a copy of
  every cleared cheque that is deposited into, the account, unless

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the account on which the cheque is drawn and the
    account into which the cheque is deposited are at the same branch of
    the financial entity, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the following conditions are met:

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} an image of the cheque has been recorded on
      microfilm or on an electronic medium,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} an image of the cheque can be readily reproduced
      from the microfilm or electronic medium,

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} it is possible to readily ascertain where the
      image of the cheque is recorded, and

    - [(D)]{.lawlabel} the microfilm or electronic medium is kept for a
      period of at least five years after the day on which the image of
      the cheque is recorded;

- [(k)]{.lawlabel} in respect of every credit arrangement that it enters
  into with a client, a record of the client's financial capacity, the
  terms of the credit arrangement, the nature of the client's principal
  business or their occupation and, if the client is a person, the name
  and address of their business or place of work;

- [(l)]{.lawlabel} a foreign currency exchange transaction ticket in
  respect of every foreign currency exchange transaction;

- [(m)]{.lawlabel} if it receives an amount of \$3,000 or more as
  consideration for the issuance of traveller's cheques, money orders or
  similar negotiable instruments from a person or entity other than
  another financial entity or a person who is acting on behalf of a
  client that is a financial entity, a record

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} of the date of the receipt,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} of the person's or entity's name and address, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} of the amount received,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} indicating whether the amount received is in funds
    or virtual currency and the type and amount of each type of funds
    and each of the virtual currencies involved,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} of the number of every account that is affected by
    the transaction, the type of account and the name of each account
    holder,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} of every reference number that is connected to the
    transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number, and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the amount received is in virtual currency, of
    every transaction identifier, including the sending and receiving
    addresses;

- [(n)]{.lawlabel} if it redeems one or more money orders that total
  \$3,000 or more at the request of a person or entity, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the redemption,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name and address, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the total amount of the money order or orders,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name of the issuer of each money order,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by the
    redemption, the type of account and the name of each account holder,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    redemption and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number, and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the redemption involves virtual currency,
    every transaction identifier, including the sending and receiving
    addresses;

- [(o)]{.lawlabel} if it initiates an international electronic funds
  transfer --- or any other electronic funds transfer that is a SWIFT
  MT-103 message or its equivalent --- of \$1,000 or more at the request
  of a person or entity, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer is
    initiated,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that is
    involved in the initiation,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name, address and
    telephone number, the nature of their principal business or their
    occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the name, and address of each beneficiary,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the initiation, the type of account and the name of each account
    holder,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
    an account number, and

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the electronic funds transfer, other than those referred to in
    subparagraph (vi);

- [(p)]{.lawlabel} if it sends an international electronic funds
  transfer of \$1,000 or more that was initiated by another person or
  entity, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer is
    sent,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if it exchanges fiat currencies in the course of
    sending the electronic funds transfer, the type and amount of each
    fiat currency that is involved in the exchange,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the electronic funds transfer, the type of account and the name of
    each account holder,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
    an account number;

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity that
    requested the initiation of the electronic funds transfer, unless
    that information was not, despite the taking of reasonable measures,
    included with the transfer and is not otherwise known,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary, unless
    that information was not, despite the taking of reasonable measures,
    included with the transfer and is not otherwise known;

- [(q)]{.lawlabel} if it is the final recipient of an international
  electronic funds transfer of \$1,000 or more, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer is
    finally received,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that is
    involved in the final receipt,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date of the remittance,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used for the remittance and
    their source,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is in funds, the type and amount
    of each type of funds involved in the remittance,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is not in funds, the type of
    remittance and its value, if different from the amount of funds
    finally received,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the final receipt or the remittance, the type of account and the
    name of each account holder,

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
    an account number,

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity that
    requested the initiation of the electronic funds transfer, unless
    that information was not, despite the taking of reasonable measures,
    included with the transfer and is not otherwise known,

  - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the electronic funds transfer, other than those referred to in
    subparagraph (viii), and

- [(r)]{.lawlabel} if it transfers an amount of \$1,000 or more in
  virtual currency at the request of a person or entity, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the transfer,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each virtual currency that
    is involved in the transfer,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name, address, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by the
    transaction, the type of account and the name of each account
    holder,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} every transaction identifier, including the
    sending and receiving addresses, and

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source,

- [(s)]{.lawlabel} if it receives an amount of \$1,000 or more in
  virtual currency for remittance to a beneficiary, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the receipt,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each virtual currency that
    is received,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date of the remittance,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used for the remittance and
    their source,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is in virtual currency, the type
    and amount of each virtual currency involved in the remittance,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is not in virtual currency, the
    type of remittance and its value, if different from the amount of
    virtual currency received,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the transaction, the type of account and the name of each account
    holder,

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number,

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} every transaction identifier, including the sending
    and receiving addresses, and

  - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity that
    requested the transfer unless that information was not, despite the
    taking of reasonable measures, included with the transfer and is not
    otherwise known;

- [(t)]{.lawlabel} a virtual currency exchange transaction ticket in
  respect of every virtual currency exchange transaction.

**[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** A financial entity shall
keep the following records in respect of every credit card account that
it opens and of every transaction that is connected to that account:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a record for each account holder and every other
  person --- up to three, in the case of a business account --- who is
  authorized to give instructions in respect of the account, containing
  their name and address, the nature of their principal business or
  their occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if an account holder is a corporation, a copy of the
  part of its official corporate records that contains any provision
  relating to the power to bind the corporation in respect of the
  account or transaction;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a record of every application in respect of the
  account;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a copy of every credit card statement that it sends
  to an account holder;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a foreign currency exchange transaction ticket in
  respect of every foreign currency exchange transaction that is
  connected to the account;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if it initiates an international electronic funds
  transfer of \$1,000 or more at the request of a person or entity and
  the funds are transferred from the account, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer is
    initiated,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that is
    involved in the initiation,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name, address and
    telephone number, the nature of their principal business or their
    occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the initiation, the type of account and the name of each account
    holder,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
    an account number, and

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the electronic funds transfer, other than those referred to in
    subparagraph (vi); and

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} if it is the final recipient of an international
  electronic funds transfer of \$1,000 or more and the remittance to the
  beneficiary is by payment to the account, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer is
    finally received,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that is
    involved in the final receipt,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date of the remittance,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used for the remittance and
    their source,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds involved
    in the remittance,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the final receipt or the remittance, the type of account and the
    name of each account holder,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
    an account number,

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity that
    requested the initiation of the electronic funds transfer, unless
    that information was not, despite the taking of reasonable measures,
    included with the transfer and is not otherwise known, and

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by the
    electronic funds transfer, other than those referred to in
    subparagraph (vii); and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} a virtual currency exchange transaction ticket in
  respect of every virtual currency exchange transaction that is
  connected to the account.

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  financial entity shall keep the following records in respect of every
  prepaid payment product account that it opens and of every transaction
  that is made by means of a prepaid payment product that is connected
  to that account:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a record of the name and address of each holder of
    a prepaid payment product account and each authorized user, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} if an account holder is a corporation, a copy of
    the part of its official corporate records that contains any
    provision relating to the power to bind the corporation in respect
    of the prepaid payment product account or the transaction;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a record of every application in respect of the
    prepaid payment product account;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a prepaid payment product slip in respect of every
    payment that is made to the prepaid payment product account;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} every debit and credit memo that it creates or
    receives in respect of the prepaid payment product account;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a copy of every account statement that it sends to
    a holder of the prepaid payment product account;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a foreign currency exchange transaction ticket in
    respect of every foreign currency exchange transaction that is
    connected to the prepaid payment product account;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if it initiates an international electronic funds
    transfer of \$1,000 or more at the request of a person or entity and
    the funds are transferred from the prepaid payment product account,
    a record of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer
      is initiated,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that
      is involved in the initiation,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name, address and
      telephone number, the nature of their principal business or their
      occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
      the initiation, the type of account and the name of each account
      holder,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
      electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
      an account number, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected
      by the electronic funds transfer, other than those referred to in
      subparagraph (vi);

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if it is the final recipient of an international
    electronic funds transfer of \$1,000 or more and the remittance to
    the beneficiary is by payment to the prepaid payment product
    account, a record of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer
      is finally received,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that
      is involved in the final receipt,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary, the
      nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
      case of a person, their date of birth,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date of the remittance,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used for the remittance and
      their source,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is in funds, the type and
      amount of each type of funds involved,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is not in funds, the type of
      remittance and its value, if different from the amount of funds
      finally received,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected
      by the final receipt or the remittance, the type of account and
      the name of each account holder,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
      electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
      an account number, and

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity that
      requested the initiation of the electronic funds transfer, unless
      that information was not, despite the taking of reasonable
      measures, included with the transfer and is not otherwise known,

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
      the electronic funds transfer, other than those referred to in
      subparagraph (viii);

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} if it transfers from a prepaid payment product
    account an amount of \$1,000 or more in virtual currency at the
    request of a person or entity, a record of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the transfer,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each virtual currency
      that is involved in the transfer,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name and address, the
      nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
      case of a person, their date of birth,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
      the transaction, the type of account and the name of each account
      holder,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
      transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
      number,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} every transaction identifier, including the
      sending and receiving addresses, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} if it receives an amount of \$1,000 or more in
    virtual currency for remittance to a beneficiary by payment to the
    prepaid payment product account, a record of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the receipt,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each virtual currency
      that is received,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary, the
      nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
      case of a person, their date of birth,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date of the remittance,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used for the remittance and
      their source,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is in virtual currency, the
      type and amount of each virtual currency involved,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is not in virtual currency,
      the type of remittance and its value, if different from the amount
      of virtual currency received,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected
      by the transaction, the type of account and the name of each
      account holder,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
      transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
      number,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} every transaction identifier, including the
      sending and receiving addresses, and

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity
      that requested the transfer unless that information was not,
      despite the taking of reasonable measures, included with the
      transfer and is not otherwise known; and

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} a virtual currency exchange transaction ticket in
    respect of every virtual currency exchange transaction that is
    connected to the prepaid payment product account.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[prepaid payment product
  slip]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a record that sets out

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the date of a payment to a prepaid payment product
    account;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person or entity that makes the
    payment;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds or each
    of the virtual currencies involved in the payment;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the method by which the payment is made;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the name of each holder of the prepaid payment
    product account; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the account number and, if different, the number
    that identifies the prepaid payment product that is connected to the
    account.

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  trust company shall also keep the following records in respect of a
  trust for which it is trustee:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the trust deed;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a record of the settlor's name and address and

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} if the settlor is a person, their date of birth
      and the nature of their principal business or their occupation,
      and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the settlor is an entity, the nature of its
      principal business; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the trust is an institutional trust and the
    settlor is a corporation, a copy of the part of the official
    corporate records that contains any provision relating to the power
    to bind the corporation in respect of the trust.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[institutional
  trust]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a trust that is established by a
  corporation or other entity for a particular business purpose and
  includes a pension plan trust, a pension master trust, a supplemental
  pension plan trust, a mutual fund trust, a pooled fund trust, a
  registered retirement savings plan trust, a registered retirement
  income fund trust, a registered education savings plan trust, a group
  registered retirement savings plan trust, a deferred profit sharing
  plan trust, an employee profit sharing plan trust, a retirement
  compensation arrangement trust, an employee savings plan trust, a
  health and welfare trust, an unemployment benefit plan trust, a
  foreign insurance company trust, a foreign reinsurance trust, a
  reinsurance trust, a real estate investment trust, an environmental
  trust and a trust established in respect of an endowment, a foundation
  or a registered charity.
