- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsections 9.4(1) and (3) of the Act,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed entity is

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an entity referred to in paragraph 5(f) of the
      Act,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a financial services cooperative,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a life insurance company, or an entity that is
      a life insurance broker or agent, in respect of loans or prepaid
      payment products that it offers to the public or accounts that it
      maintains with respect to those loans or prepaid payment products,
      other than

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} loans that are made by the insurer to a policy
        holder if the insured person has a terminal illness that
        significantly reduces their life expectancy and the loan is
        secured by the value of an insurance policy,

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} loans that are made by the insurer to the
        policy holder for the sole purpose of funding the life insurance
        policy, and

      - [(C)]{.lawlabel} advance payments to which the policy holder is
        entitled that are made to them by the insurer,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a credit union central when it offers financial
      services to a person, or to an entity that is not a member of that
      credit union central, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a department, or an entity that is an agent of
      His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His
      Majesty in right of a province, when it accepts deposit
      liabilities in the course of providing financial services to the
      public; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a prescribed foreign entity is a foreign financial
    institution.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A financial entity shall, when it enters into a
  correspondent banking relationship, keep the following records:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a record of the foreign financial institution's
    name and address, its primary business line and the names of its
    directors;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the foreign financial institution's most
    recent annual report or audited financial statement;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the foreign financial institution's
    banking licence, banking charter, authorization or certification to
    operate issued by the competent authority under the legislation of
    the jurisdiction in which the foreign financial institution was
    incorporated, of its certificate of incorporation or of a similar
    document;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a copy of the correspondent banking agreement or
    arrangement, or product agreements, defining the respective
    responsibilities of the financial entity and the foreign financial
    institution;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a record of the anticipated correspondent banking
    account activity of the foreign financial institution, including the
    products or services to be used;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a written statement from the foreign financial
    institution that it does not have, directly or indirectly, a
    correspondent banking relationship with a shell bank;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a written statement from the foreign financial
    institution that it is in compliance with anti-money laundering and
    anti-terrorist financing legislation in every jurisdiction in which
    it operates;

  - [(g.1)]{.lawlabel} a record of measures taken to determine the
    nature of the clientele and markets served by the foreign financial
    institution;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} a record of the measures taken to ascertain whether
    any civil or criminal penalties have been imposed on the foreign
    financial institution in respect of anti-money laundering or
    anti-terrorist financing requirements and the results of those
    measures;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a record of the measures taken to assess the
    reputation of the foreign financial institution with respect to its
    compliance with anti-money laundering and anti-terrorist financing
    requirements and the results of those measures; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} a record of measures taken to assess the quality of
    the anti-money laundering and anti-terrorist financing supervision
    of the jurisdiction in which the foreign financial institution was
    incorporated and the jurisdiction in which it conducts transactions
    in the context of the correspondent banking relationship and the
    results of those measures.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The financial entity shall take reasonable measures
  to ascertain whether the foreign financial institution has in place
  anti-money laundering and anti-terrorist financing policies and
  procedures, including procedures for approval for the opening of new
  accounts and, if the reasonable measures are unsuccessful or the
  policies and procedures are not in place, shall, for the purpose of
  detecting any transactions that are required to be reported to the
  Centre under section 7 of the Act, take reasonable measures to monitor
  all transactions conducted in the context of the correspondent banking
  relationship.

- [(3.1)]{.lawlabel} A financial entity that enters into a correspondent
  banking relationship shall periodically conduct, at a frequency that
  is appropriate to the level of risk, based on a risk assessment
  referred to in subsection 9.6(2) of the Act that takes into account
  the information collected in respect of the foreign financial
  institution in accordance with the Act and these Regulations, ongoing
  monitoring of that correspondent banking relationship for the purpose
  of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} detecting any transactions that are required to be
    reported in accordance with section 7 of the Act;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} keeping the information referred to in
    subsections (2) and (3) and sections 90 and 91 of these Regulations
    up to date;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} reassessing the level of risk associated with the
    foreign financial institution's transactions and activities related
    to the correspondent banking relationship; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} determining whether transactions or activities are
    consistent with the information obtained about the foreign financial
    institution and with the risk assessment.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, section 12 does not apply in
  respect of an account that is opened for a foreign financial
  institution in the context of a correspondent banking relationship.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 28](#1179452-1294375){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179452-1294375.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 3](#1421256-1492439){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421256-1492439.html"}

### [Life Insurance Companies and Life Insurance Brokers or Agents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1294376 .Subheading}

- **[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A life
  insurance broker or agent is engaged in a business or profession for
  the purposes of paragraph 5(i) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a life insurance
  broker or agent when they are acting as a managing general agent.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-122, s. 30
- SOR/2016-153, s. 24
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 28](#1179452-1294380){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179452-1294380.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-17-20170617.html "Link to previous version of section 17"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** A life insurance company
or life insurance broker or agent that receives an amount of \$10,000 or
more in cash from a person or entity in a single transaction shall
report the transaction to the Centre, together with the information set
out in Schedule 1, unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount is received from a financial entity or
  public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a client that
  is a financial entity or public body; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the transaction

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} involves the sale of an immediate or deferred
    annuity that is paid for entirely with funds that are directly
    transferred from a registered pension plan or from a pension plan
    that is required to be registered under the [Pension Benefits
    Standards Act, 1985](/eng/acts/P-7.01), or similar provincial
    legislation,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} involves the sale of a registered annuity policy
    or a registered retirement income fund,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} involves the sale of an immediate or deferred
    annuity that is paid for entirely with the proceeds of a group life
    insurance policy, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} is part of a reverse mortgage or structured
    settlement.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-122, s. 31
- SOR/2016-153, s. 25
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 28](#1179452-1294388){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179452-1294388.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-18-20170617.html "Link to previous version of section 18"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** A life insurance company
or life insurance broker or agent that receives an amount of \$10,000 or
more in virtual currency from a person or entity in a single transaction
shall report the transaction to the Centre, together with the
information set out in Schedule 4.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-122, s. 32
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 28](#1179452-1294390){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179452-1294390.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-19-20080623.html "Link to previous version of section 19"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** A life insurance company
or life insurance broker or agent shall keep a large cash transaction
record in respect of every amount of \$10,000 or more in cash that they
receive from a person or entity in a single transaction, unless

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the amount is received from a financial entity or
  public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a client that
  is a financial entity or public body; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the transaction

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} involves the sale of an immediate or deferred
    annuity that is paid for entirely with funds that are directly
    transferred from a registered pension plan or from a pension plan
    that is required to be registered under the [Pension Benefits
    Standards Act, 1985](/eng/acts/P-7.01), or similar provincial
    legislation,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} involves the sale of a registered annuity policy
    or a registered retirement income fund,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} involves the sale of an immediate or deferred
    annuity that is paid for entirely with the proceeds of a group life
    insurance policy, or

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} is part of a reverse mortgage or structured
    settlement.

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A life
  insurance company or life insurance broker or agent shall, regardless
  of the means of payment, keep an information record in connection with
  the sale of an immediate or deferred annuity or a life insurance
  policy

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} for which they are to receive an amount of \$10,000
    or more over the duration of the annuity or policy; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} under which they are to remit an amount of \$10,000
    or more to a beneficiary over the duration of the annuity or policy.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The information record

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in connection with the sale referred to in
    paragraph (1)(a) shall be created when the life insurance company or
    life insurance broker or agent establishes the annuity or policy and
    shall be kept, subject to subsection (3), in respect of the
    annuitant or policy holder; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in connection with the sale referred to in
    paragraph (1)(b) shall be created before the life insurance company
    or life insurance broker or agent first remits funds or virtual
    currency to the beneficiary under the annuity or policy and shall be
    kept in respect of the beneficiary.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a life insurance policy that is a
  group life insurance policy or in the case of a group annuity, the
  information record shall be kept in respect of the applicant for the
  policy or annuity.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Paragraph (2)(b) does not apply if the life insurance
  company or life insurance broker or agent, due to facts or
  circumstances beyond its control, is unable to create the information
  record within the time period in which they are required, under
  federal or provincial legislation, to first remit funds or virtual
  currency to the beneficiary.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 28](#1179452-1294415){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179452-1294415.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-22-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 22"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** A life insurance company
or life insurance broker or agent that keeps an information record under
section 22 in respect of a corporation shall also keep a copy of the
part of the official corporate records that contains any provision
relating to the power to bind the corporation in respect of transactions
with the life insurance company or life insurance broker or agent.

Version](section-23-20170617.html "Link to previous version of section 23"){.PITLink}

apply to a life insurance company or life insurance broker or agent when
they are dealing in reinsurance.

### [Factors]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1526896 .Subheading}

**[[24.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.1 .sectionLabel}** A factor is engaged
in a business or profession for the purposes of paragraph 5(i) of the
Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526899){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526899.html"}
:::

- **[[24.11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.11
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A factor shall report the following
  transactions and information to the Centre:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the receipt from a person or entity of an amount of
    \$10,000 or more in cash in a single transaction, together with the
    information set out in Schedule 1; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the receipt from a person or entity of an amount of
    \$10,000 or more in virtual currency in a single transaction,
    together with the information set out in Schedule 4.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A factor is not required to report the transaction
  and information under paragraph (1)(a) if the amount is received from
  a financial entity or public body or from a person who is acting on
  behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526906){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526906.html"}
:::

**[[24.12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.12 .sectionLabel}** A factor shall keep
a large cash transaction record in respect of every amount of \$10,000
or more in cash that it receives from a person or entity in a single
transaction, unless the amount is received from a financial entity or
public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a client that is
a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526909){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526909.html"}
:::

**[[24.13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.13 .sectionLabel}** A factor shall keep
a large virtual currency transaction record in respect of every amount
of \$10,000 or more in virtual currency that it receives from a person
or entity in a single transaction, unless the amount is received from a
financial entity or public body or from a person who is acting on behalf
of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526912){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526912.html"}
:::

**[[24.14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.14 .sectionLabel}** A factor shall keep
the following records in respect of every factoring agreement that it
enters into:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an information record in respect of the person or
  entity with whom it enters into the agreement;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the information record is in respect of an entity,
  a record of the name, address and date of birth of every person who
  enters into the agreement on behalf of the entity and the nature of
  the person's principal business or their occupation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the information record is in respect of a
  corporation, a copy of the part of official corporate records that
  contains any provision relating to the power to bind the corporation
  in respect of transactions with the factor;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a record of the financial capacity of the person or
  entity with which it enters into the agreement and the terms of the
  agreement;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} for any payment it makes, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the payment,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the payment is in funds, the type and amount of
    each type of funds involved,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} if the payment is not in funds, the type of
    payment and its value,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the method by which the payment is made,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the name of every person or entity involved in the
    payment, and

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} every account number or other equivalent reference
    number connected to the payment; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} a receipt of funds record in respect of every amount
  of \$3,000 or more that it receives, unless the amount is received
  from a financial entity or public body or from a person who is acting
  on behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526927){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526927.html"}
:::

### [Financing or Leasing Entities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1526928 .Subheading}

**[[24.15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.15 .sectionLabel}** A financing or
leasing entity is engaged in a business or profession for the purposes
of paragraph 5(j) of the Act when it is financing or leasing

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} property, other than real property or immovables, for
  business purposes;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} passenger vehicles in Canada; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} property, other than real property or immovables,
  that is valued at \$100,000 or more.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526934){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526934.html"}
:::

**[[24.16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.16 .sectionLabel}** A financing or
leasing entity that receives an amount of \$10,000 or more in cash in a
single transaction in connection with an activity referred to in section
24.15 shall report the transaction to the Centre, together with the
information set out in Schedule 1, unless the amount is received from a
financial entity or public body or from a person who is acting on behalf
of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526937){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526937.html"}
:::

**[[24.17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.17 .sectionLabel}** A financing or
leasing entity that receives an amount of \$10,000 or more in virtual
currency in a single transaction in connection with an activity referred
to in section 24.15 shall report the transaction to the Centre, together
with the information set out in Schedule 4.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526940){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526940.html"}
:::

**[[24.18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.18 .sectionLabel}** A financing or
leasing entity shall keep a large cash transaction record in respect of
every amount of \$10,000 or more in cash that it receives in a single
transaction in connection with an activity referred to in section 24.15,
unless the amount is received from a financial entity or public body or
from a person who is acting on behalf of a client that is a financial
entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526943){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526943.html"}
:::

**[[24.19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.19 .sectionLabel}** A financing or
leasing entity shall keep a large virtual currency transaction record in
respect of every amount of \$10,000 or more in virtual currency that it
receives in a single transaction in connection with an activity referred
to in section 24.15, unless the amount is received from a financial
entity or public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a
client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526946){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526946.html"}
:::

**[[24.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24.2 .sectionLabel}** A financing or
leasing entity shall keep the following records in connection with an
activity referred to in section 24.15 in respect of every financing or
leasing arrangement that it enters into:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} an information record in respect of the person or
  entity with which it enters into the arrangement;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the information record is in respect of an entity,
  a record of the name, address and date of birth of every person who
  enters into the arrangement on behalf of the entity and the nature of
  the person's principal business or their occupation;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the information record is in respect of a
  corporation, a copy of the part of official corporate records that
  contains any provision relating to the power to bind the corporation
  in respect of transactions with the financial or leasing entity;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a record of the financial capacity of the person or
  entity with which it enters into the arrangement and the terms of the
  arrangement; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} in respect of every payment that it receives under
  the arrangement, other than a payment received from a financial entity
  or public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a client
  that is a financial entity or public body, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the payment,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person or entity that makes the
    payment,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the amount of the payment and of any part of it
    that is made in cash, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the method by which the payment is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 3](#1517542-1526958){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517542-1526958.html"}
:::

### [Securities Dealers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1294421 .Subheading}

receives an amount of \$10,000 or more in cash from a person or entity
in a single transaction shall report the transaction to the Centre,

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** A securities dealer shall
keep the following records in respect of every account that they open:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} every signature card;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a record for each account holder and every other
  person --- up to three, in the case of a business account --- who is
  authorized to give instructions in respect of the account, containing
  their name and address, the nature of their principal business or
  their occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if an account holder is a corporation, a copy of the
  part of its official corporate records that contains any provision
  relating to the power to bind the corporation in respect of the
  account;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a record that sets out the intended use of the
  account;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a record of every application in respect of the
  account;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} every account operating agreement that they create or
  receive in respect of the account;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} every confirmation of purchase or sale, guarantee,
  trade authorization, power of attorney and joint account agreement and
  all correspondence that pertains to the operation of the account; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} a copy of every account statement that they send to
  an account holder.

### [Money Services Businesses and Foreign Money Services Businesses]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1294445 .Subheading}

**[[29.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29.1 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
subparagraphs 5(h)(v) and (h.1)(v) of the Act, crowdfunding platform
services and cheque-cashing services are prescribed services.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-76, s. 2](#1343097-1343262){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343097-1343262.html"}

:::

Version](section-29.1-20220405.html "Link to previous version of section 29.1"){.PITLink}

- **[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  money services business shall report the following transactions and
  information to the Centre:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the receipt from a person or entity of an amount of
    \$10,000 or more in cash in a single transaction, together with the
    information set out in Schedule 1;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the initiation, at the request of a person or
    entity, of an international electronic funds transfer of \$10,000 or
    more in a single transaction, together with the information set out
    in Schedule 2;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the final receipt of an international electronic
    funds transfer of \$10,000 or more in a single transaction, together
    with the information set out in Schedule 3;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the initiation, at the request of a person or
    entity, of an international electronic funds transfer of \$10,000 or
    more in a single transaction, together with the information set out
    in Schedule 2, if the money services business also finally receives
    or is to finally receive the electronic funds transfer;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the final receipt of an international electronic
    funds transfer of \$10,000 or more in a single transaction, together
    with the information set out in Schedule 3, if the money services
    business also initiated the electronic funds transfer; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the receipt from a person or entity of an amount of
    \$10,000 or more in virtual currency in a single transaction,
    together with the information set out in Schedule 4.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A money services business is not required to report
  the transaction and information under paragraph (1)(a) if the amount
  is received from a financial entity or public body or from a person
  who is acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity or
  public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-122, ss. 37, 76
- SOR/2008-21, s. 6

  ajax-after="1179452-1294455.html"}
:::

Version](section-30-20170617.html "Link to previous version of section 30"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** A money services business
shall keep a large cash transaction record in respect of every amount of
\$10,000 or more in cash that they receive from a person or entity in a
single transaction, unless the amount is received from a financial
entity or public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a
client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-122, s. 38
- SOR/2016-153, s. 29
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 28](#1179452-1294457){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179452-1294457.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-31-20170617.html "Link to previous version of section 31"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[32]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32 .sectionLabel}** A money services business
shall keep a large virtual currency transaction record in respect of
every amount of \$10,000 or more in virtual currency that they receive
from a person or entity in a single transaction, unless the amount is
received from a financial entity or public body or from a person who is
acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-122, s. 38
- SOR/2016-153, s. 81(F)

Version](section-32-20160630.html "Link to previous version of section 32"){.PITLink}

**[[32.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-32.1 .sectionLabel}** A money services
business is not required to report the transaction or information
referred to in paragraph 30(1)(a) or (f) or to keep the record referred
to in section 31 or 32 if the cash or virtual currency is received only
for the purpose of transport

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to or from a person or entity referred to in section
  5 of the Act, if the transport is carried out at the request of such a
  person or entity and the cash or virtual currency is of an amount that
  is not declared to the money services business and that they cannot
  readily determine;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} between the Bank of Canada and a person or entity in
  Canada; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} between two places of business of the same person or
  entity, if that person or entity is referred to in section 5 of the
  Act and the transport is carried out at their request.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-193, s. 8](#1421148-1485958){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421148-1485958.html"}
:::

- **[[33]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-33 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  foreign money services business shall report the following
  transactions and information to the Centre:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the receipt from a person or entity in Canada of an
    amount of \$10,000 or more in cash in a single transaction, together
    with the information set out in Schedule 1;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the initiation, at the request of a person or
    entity in Canada, of an electronic funds transfer of \$10,000 or
    more in a single transaction, together with the information set out
    in Schedule 2, if the electronic funds transfer is sent or is to be
    sent from one country to another;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the final receipt of an electronic funds transfer
    of \$10,000 or more in a single transaction, together with the
    information set out in Schedule 3, if the electronic funds transfer
    was sent from one country to another and the beneficiary is in
    Canada;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the initiation, at the request of a person or
    entity in Canada, of an international electronic funds transfer of
    \$10,000 or more in a single transaction, together with the
    information set out in Schedule 2, if the foreign money services
    business also finally receives or is to finally receive the
    electronic funds transfer;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the final receipt of an international electronic
    funds transfer of \$10,000 or more in a single transaction, together
    with the information set out in Schedule 3, if the foreign money
    services business also initiated the electronic funds transfer and
    the beneficiary is in Canada; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} the receipt from a person or entity in Canada of an
    amount of \$10,000 or more in virtual currency in a single
    transaction, together with the information set out in Schedule 4.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A foreign money services business is not required to
  report the transaction and information under paragraph (1)(a) if the
  amount is received from a financial entity or public body or from a
  person who is acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity
  or public body.

**[[34]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-34 .sectionLabel}** A foreign money services
business shall keep a large cash transaction record in respect of every
amount of \$10,000 or more in cash that they receive from a person or
entity in Canada in a single transaction, unless the amount is received
from a financial entity or public body or from a person who is acting on
behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

**[[35]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35 .sectionLabel}** A foreign money services
business shall keep a large virtual currency transaction record in
respect of every amount of \$10,000 or more in virtual currency that
they receive from a person or entity in Canada in a single transaction,
unless the amount is received from a financial entity or public body or
from a person who is acting on behalf of a client that is a financial
entity or public body.

**[[35.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-35.1 .sectionLabel}** A foreign money
services business is not required to report the transaction or
information referred to in paragraph 33(1)(a) or (f) or to keep the
record referred to in section 34 or 35 if the cash or virtual currency
is received only for the purpose of transport

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} to or from a person or entity referred to in section
  5 of the Act, if the transport is carried out at the request of such a
  person or entity and the cash or virtual currency is of an amount that
  is not declared to the foreign money services business and that they
  cannot readily determine;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} between the Bank of Canada and a person or entity in
  Canada; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} between two places of business of the same person or
  entity, if that person or entity is referred to in section 5 of the
  Act and the transport is carried out at their request.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-193, s. 9](#1421150-1485964){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421150-1485964.html"}
:::

**[[36]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36 .sectionLabel}** A money services business
shall keep the following records in connection with a service referred
to in any of subparagraphs 5(h)(i) to (v) of the Act that they provide,
and a foreign money services business shall keep the following records
in connection with a service referred to in any of subparagraphs
5(h.1)(i) to (v) of the Act that they provide to persons or entities in
Canada:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} every internal memorandum that they create or receive
  that concerns the service;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they receive an amount of \$3,000 or more from a
  person or entity --- other than a financial entity or a person who is
  acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity --- as
  consideration for the issuance of traveller's cheques, money orders or
  similar negotiable instruments, a record

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} of the date of the receipt,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} of the person's or entity's name and address, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} of the amount received,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} indicating whether the amount received is in funds
    or virtual currency and the type and amount of each type of funds
    and each of the virtual currencies involved,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} of the number of every account that is affected by
    the transaction, the type of account and the name of each account
    holder,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} of every reference number that is connected to the
    transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number, and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the amount received is in virtual currency, of
    every transaction identifier, including the sending and receiving
    addresses;

- [(b.1)]{.lawlabel} if they cash one or more cheques that total \$3,000
  or more at the request of a person or entity, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date when each cheque is cashed,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name and address, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the total amount of the cheque or cheques,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name of the issuer of each cheque,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by the
    cashing of the cheque or cheques, the type of account and the name
    of each account holder,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    cashing of the cheque or cheques and that has a function equivalent
    to that of an account number, and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the cashing of the cheque or cheques involves
    virtual currency, every transaction identifier, including the
    sending and receiving addresses;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if they redeem one or more money orders that total
  \$3,000 or more at the request of a person or entity, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the redemption,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name and address, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the total amount of the money order or orders,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name of the issuer of each money order,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by the
    redemption, the type of account and the name of each account holder,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    redemption and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number, and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the redemption involves virtual currency,
    every transaction identifier, including the sending and receiving
    addresses;

- [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} if they transmit an amount of \$1,000 or more in
  funds at the request of a person or entity, other than in the case of
  an electronic funds transfer, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the transmission,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that is
    involved in the transmission,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name, address and
    telephone number, the nature of their principal business or their
    occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the transaction, and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number;

- [(c.2)]{.lawlabel} if they remit an amount of \$1,000 or more in funds
  to a beneficiary at the request of a person or entity, other than in
  the case of an electronic funds transfer, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the remittance,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that is
    involved in the remittance,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the person or entity who requested
    the remittance,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name, address and telephone number of each
    beneficiary, the nature of their principal business or their
    occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used for the remittance and
    their source,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the transaction, and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if they initiate an electronic funds transfer of
  \$1,000 or more at the request of a person or entity, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer is
    initiated,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that is
    involved in the initiation,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name, address and
    telephone number, the nature of their principal business or their
    occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the initiation, the type of account and the name of each account
    holder,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
    an account number, and

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the electronic funds transfer, other than those referred to in
    subparagraph (vi);

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if they send an international electronic funds
  transfer of \$1,000 or more that was initiated by another person or
  entity, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer is
    sent,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if they exchange fiat currencies in the course of
    sending the electronic funds transfer, the type and amount of each
    fiat currency that is involved in the exchange,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the sending, the type of account and the name of each account
    holder,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    sending and has a function equivalent to that of an account number,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity that
    requested the initiation of the electronic funds transfer, unless
    that information was not, despite the taking of reasonable measures,
    included with the transfer and is not otherwise known, and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary, unless
    that information was not, despite the taking of reasonable measures,
    included with the transfer and is not otherwise known;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if they are the final recipient of an international
  electronic funds transfer of \$1,000 or more, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer is
    finally received,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that is
    involved in the final receipt,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name, address and telephone number of each
    beneficiary, the nature of their principal business or their
    occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date of the remittance,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used for the remittance and
    their source,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is in funds, the type and amount
    of each type of funds involved,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is not in funds, the type of
    remittance and its value, if different from the amount of funds
    finally received,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the final receipt or the remittance, the type of account and the
    name of each account holder,

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
    an account number,

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity that
    requested the initiation of the electronic funds transfer, unless
    that information was not, despite the taking of reasonable measures,
    included with the transfer and is not otherwise known,

  - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the electronic funds transfer, other than those referred to in
    subparagraph (viii);

- [(f.1)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraphs (f.2) and (f.3), if, at the
  request of a person or entity, they transport \$1,000 or more in cash
  or [[virtual currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within the meaning of
  paragraph (b) of the definition of that term in subsection 1(2) or
  \$3,000 or more in money orders, traveller's cheques or other similar
  negotiable instruments, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date and location of collection and delivery,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of cash, virtual currency or
    negotiable instruments transported,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity that
    made the request, the nature of their principal business or their
    occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name and address, if known, of each
    beneficiary,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by the
    transport, the type of account and the name of each account holder,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    transport and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number, and

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the method of remittance;

- [(f.2)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraph (f.3), if, at the request of a
  person or entity, they transport cash, [[virtual
  currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within the meaning of paragraph (b)
  of the definition of that term in subsection 1(2) or money orders,
  traveller's cheques or other similar negotiable instruments in an
  amount that has not been declared and that they cannot readily
  determine, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date and location of collection and delivery,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type, if known, of cash, virtual currency or
    negotiable instruments transported,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name, address and telephone number of the
    person or entity that made the request, the nature of their
    principal business or their occupation and, in the case of a person,
    their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name and address, if known, of each
    beneficiary,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by the
    transport, the type of account and the name of each account holder,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    transport and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the reason why the amount was not declared, and

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the method of remittance;

- [(f.3)]{.lawlabel} if, at the request of an entity referred to in
  paragraph 5(a) or (b) of the Act, they transport cash, [[virtual
  currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within the meaning of paragraph (b)
  of the definition of that term in subsection 1(2) or money orders,
  traveller's cheques or other similar negotiable instruments, a record
  of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date and location of collection and delivery,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount, if known, of cash, virtual
    currency or negotiable instruments transported, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name, address and telephone number of the
    entity that made the request;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} if they transfer an amount of \$1,000 or more in
  virtual currency at the request of a person or entity, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the transfer,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each virtual currency that
    is involved in the transfer,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name and address, the
    nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
    case of a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by the
    transaction, the type of account and the name of each account
    holder, and

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} every transaction identifier, including the
    sending and receiving addresses, and

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} if they receive an amount of \$1,000 or more in
  virtual currency for remittance to a beneficiary, a record of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date of the receipt,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each virtual currency that
    is received,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name, address of each beneficiary, the nature
    of their principal business or their occupation and, in the case of
    a person, their date of birth,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date of the remittance,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used for the remittance and
    their source,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is in virtual currency, the type
    and amount of each virtual currency involved,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is not in virtual currency, the
    type of remittance and its value, if different from the amount of
    virtual currency received,

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
    the transaction, the type of account and the name of each account
    holder,

  - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
    transaction and has a function equivalent to that of an account
    number,

  - [(x)]{.lawlabel} every transaction identifier, including the sending
    and receiving addresses, and

  - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity that
    requested the transfer unless that information was not, despite the
    taking of reasonable measures, included with the transfer and is not
    otherwise known;

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} a foreign currency exchange transaction ticket in
  respect of every foreign currency exchange transaction; and

- [(j)]{.lawlabel} a virtual currency exchange transaction ticket in
  respect of every virtual currency exchange transaction.

**[[36.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-36.1 .sectionLabel}** A money services
business, in connection with crowdfunding platform services that they
provide, and a foreign money services business, in connection with
crowdfunding platform services that they provide to persons or entities
in Canada, shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} keep an information record in respect of the person
  or entity to which they provide those services;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} keep a record of the purpose for which the funds or
  virtual currency are being raised; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the person or entity for which the funds or
  virtual currency are being raised is different from the person or
  entity referred to in paragraph (a),

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} keep a record of their name, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} take reasonable measures to obtain their address,
    the nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in
    the case of a person, their date of birth, and keep a record of the
    information obtained.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-76, s. 3](#1343105-1343270){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343105-1343270.html"}
:::

**[[37]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-37 .sectionLabel}** A money services business
that enters into an agreement with an entity to provide a service
referred to in any of subparagraphs 5(h)(i) to (v) of the Act to that
entity --- and a foreign money services business that enters into an
agreement with an entity in Canada to provide a service referred to in
any of subparagraphs 5(h.1)(i) to (v) of the Act to that entity ---
shall keep

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a record of the name, address and date of birth of
  every person who signs the agreement on behalf of the entity and the
  nature of the person's principal business or their occupation;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an information record in respect of the entity;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the entity is a corporation, a copy of the part of
  its official corporate records that contains any provision relating to
  the power to bind the corporation in respect of transactions with the
  money services business or foreign money services business; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} a list containing the name, address and date of birth
  of every employee of the entity who is authorized to order a
  transaction under the agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 28](#1179452-1294677){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179452-1294677.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-37-20080623.html "Link to previous version of section 37"){.PITLink}
:::

### [British Columbia Notaries Public and British Columbia Notary Corporations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1294678 .Subheading}

- **[[38]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-38 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  British Columbia notary public or British Columbia notary corporation
  is engaged in a business or profession for the purposes of paragraph
  5(j) of the Act when, on behalf of a person or entity, they

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} receive or pay funds or virtual currency, other
    than in respect of professional fees, disbursements, expenses or
    bail;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} purchase or sell securities, real property or
    immovables or business assets or entities;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} transfer funds, virtual currency or securities by
    any means; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} give instructions in connection with an activity
    referred to in any of paragraphs (a) to (c).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to a British Columbia
  notary public who is acting in the capacity of an employee.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 28](#1179452-1294687){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179452-1294687.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-38-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 38"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[39]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-39 .sectionLabel}** A British Columbia notary
public or British Columbia notary corporation that receives an amount of
\$10,000 or more in cash in a single transaction in connection with an
activity referred to in section 38 shall report the transaction to the
Centre, together with the information set out in Schedule 1, unless the
amount is received from a financial entity or public body or from a
person who is acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity or
public body.

::: HistoricalNote

  ajax-after="1179452-1294690.html"}
:::

:::

**[[40]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-40 .sectionLabel}** A British Columbia notary
public or British Columbia notary corporation that receives an amount of
\$10,000 or more in virtual currency in a single transaction in
connection with an activity referred to in section 38 shall report the
transaction to the Centre, together with the information set out in
Schedule 4.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 28](#1179452-1294693){.wb-lbx

public or British Columbia notary corporation shall keep a large cash
transaction record in respect of every amount of \$10,000 or more in
cash that they receive in a single transaction in connection with an
activity referred to in section 38, unless the amount is received from a

public or British Columbia notary corporation shall keep a large virtual
currency transaction record in respect of every amount of \$10,000 or
more in virtual currency that they receive in a single transaction in

**[[43]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-43 .sectionLabel}** A British Columbia notary
public or British Columbia notary corporation shall keep the following
records in connection with an activity referred to in section 38:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a receipt of funds record in respect of every amount
  of \$3,000 or more that they receive, unless the amount is received
  from a financial entity or public body or from a person who is acting
  on behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the receipt of funds record is in respect of a
  corporation, a copy of the part of official corporate records that
  contains any provision relating to the power to bind the corporation
  in respect of transactions with the British Columbia notary public or
  British Columbia notary corporation.

### [Legal Counsel and Legal Firms]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1297436 .Subheading}

- **[[44]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-44
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection (2), every
  legal counsel and every legal firm is subject to Part 1 of the Act
  when they engage in any of the following activities on behalf of any
  person or entity:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} receiving or paying funds, other than those
    received or paid in respect of professional fees, disbursements,
    expenses or bail; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} giving instructions in respect of any activity
    referred to in paragraph (a).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of legal
  counsel when they engage in any of the activities referred to in that
  subsection on behalf of their employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-293, s. 14
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 29](#1179454-1297524){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179454-1297524.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-44-20090928.html "Link to previous version of section 44"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[45]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-45 .sectionLabel}** Subject to subsection
62(2), every legal counsel and every legal firm shall, when engaging in
an activity described in section 33.3, keep the following records:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a receipt of funds record in respect of every amount
  of \$3,000 or more that they receive in the course of a single
  transaction, unless the amount is received from a financial entity or
  a public body; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} where the receipt of funds record is in respect of a
  client that is a corporation, a copy of the part of official corporate
  records that contains any provision relating to the power to bind the
  corporation in respect of transactions with the legal counsel or legal
  firm.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-293, s. 14
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 30](#1179455-1297530){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179455-1297530.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-45-20160630.html "Link to previous version of section 45"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[46]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-46 .sectionLabel}** A legal counsel or legal
firm that, in connection with a transaction, receives funds from the
trust account of a legal firm or from the trust account of a legal
counsel who is not acting on behalf of their employer,

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} must keep and retain a record of that fact; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} is not required to include in the receipt of funds
  record that is kept in respect of those funds

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the number and type of any account that is affected
    by the transaction, or

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the full name of the person or entity that is the
    holder of that account.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-293, s. 14
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 30](#1179455-1297538){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179455-1297538.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-46-20160630.html "Link to previous version of section 46"){.PITLink}
:::

### [Accountants and Accounting Firms]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295546 .Subheading}

- **[[47]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-47 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} An
  accountant or accounting firm is engaged in a business or profession
  for the purposes of paragraph 5(j) of the Act when, on behalf of a
  person or entity, they

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} receive or pay funds or virtual currency;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} purchase or sell securities, real property or
    immovables or business assets or entities;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} transfer funds, virtual currency or securities by
    any means; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} give instructions in connection with an activity
    referred to in any of paragraphs (a) to (c).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the activities in
  subsection (1) do not include activities that are carried out in the
  course of an audit, a review or a compilation engagement within the
  meaning of the CPA Canada Handbook prepared and published by the
  Chartered Professional Accountants of Canada, as amended from time to
  time.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply to an accountant who is
  acting in the capacity of an employee or of a person who either is
  authorized by law to carry on the business of --- or to monitor the
  business or financial affairs of --- an insolvent or bankrupt person
  or entity or is authorized to act under a security agreement.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2016-153, s. 35(E)
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295555){.wb-lbx

:::

### [Real Estate Brokers or Sales Representatives]{.HTitleText2} {#h-683224 .Subheading}

sales representative is engaged in a business or profession for the
purposes of paragraph 5(j) of the Act when they act as an agent or

- **[[58]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-58 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A real
  estate broker or sales representative shall keep the following records
  in connection with an activity referred to in section 53:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a receipt of funds record in respect of every
    amount that they receive, unless the amount is received from a
    financial entity or public body or from a person who is acting on
    behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an information record in respect of every person or
    entity for which they act as an agent or mandatary in respect of the
    purchase or sale of real property or immovables; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the receipt of funds record or information
    record is in respect of a corporation, a copy of the part of
    official corporate records that contains any provision relating to
    the power to bind the corporation in respect of transactions with
    the real estate broker or sales representative.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If two or more of the parties to a transaction are
  represented by a real estate broker or sales representative and one of
  those real estate brokers or sales representatives receives funds in
  respect of the transaction from a party that is represented by another
  real estate broker or sales representative, the real estate broker or
  sales representative that represents the party from which the funds
  are received is responsible for keeping the receipt of funds record
  referred to in paragraph (1)(a) and, if applicable, the copy referred
  to in paragraph (1)(c).

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A real estate broker or sales representative that is
  responsible under subsection (2) for keeping a receipt of funds record
  is not required to include in that record any of the following
  information if, after taking reasonable measures to do so, they are
  unable to obtain it:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number and type of an account that is affected
    by the transaction referred to in subsection (2);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of a holder of the account;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a reference number that is connected to the
    transaction.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A real estate broker or sales representative that is
  responsible under subsection (2) for keeping a receipt of funds record
  and that determines that the transaction affects a trust account held
  by another real estate broker or sales representative shall include
  that information in the record but is not required to include

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the number of the trust account; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the name of the holder or holders of the trust
    account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295618){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179465-1295618.html"}
:::

### [Real Estate Developers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-683341 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote

**[[59]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-59 .sectionLabel}** A real estate developer
is engaged in a business or profession for the purposes of paragraph
5(j) of the Act when they sell a new house, new condominium unit, new
commercial or industrial building or new multi-unit residential building
to the public, including, if they are a corporation, when they do so on
behalf of a subsidiary or affiliate.

**[[64]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64 .sectionLabel}** A real estate developer
shall keep the following records in connection with an activity referred
to in section 59:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a receipt of funds record in respect of every amount
  that they receive, unless the amount is received from a financial
  entity or public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a
  client that is a financial entity or public body;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an information record in respect of every person or
  entity to which they sell a new house, new condominium unit, new
  commercial or industrial building or new multi-unit residential
  building; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the receipt of funds record or information record
  is in respect of a corporation, a copy of the part of official
  corporate records that contains any provision relating to the power to
  bind the corporation in respect of transactions with the real estate
  developer.

### [Mortgage Administrators, Mortgage Brokers and Mortgage Lenders]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1492441 .Subheading}

- **[[64.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  mortgage administrator is engaged in a business or profession for the
  purposes of paragraph 5(i) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A mortgage broker is engaged in a business or
  profession for the purposes of paragraph 5(i) of the Act.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A mortgage lender is engaged in a business or
  profession for the purposes of paragraph 5(i) of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 4](#1421265-1492447){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421265-1492447.html"}
:::

[Previous
Version](section-64.1-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 64.1"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[64.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64.2 .sectionLabel}** A mortgage
administrator, mortgage broker or mortgage lender that receives an
amount of \$10,000 or more in cash in a single transaction shall report
the transaction to the Centre, together with the information set out in
Schedule 1, unless the amount is received from a financial entity or
public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a client that is
a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 4](#1421265-1492450){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421265-1492450.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-64.2-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 64.2"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[64.3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64.3 .sectionLabel}** A mortgage
administrator, mortgage broker or mortgage lender that receives an
amount of \$10,000 or more in virtual currency in a single transaction
shall report the transaction to the Centre, together with the
information set out in Schedule 4, unless the amount is received from a
financial entity or public body or from a person who is acting on behalf
of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 4](#1421265-1492453){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421265-1492453.html"}
:::

**[[64.4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64.4 .sectionLabel}** A mortgage
administrator, mortgage broker or mortgage lender shall keep a large
cash transaction record in respect of every amount of \$10,000 or more
in cash that they receive in a single transaction, unless the amount is
received from a financial entity or public body or from a person who is
acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 4](#1421265-1492456){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421265-1492456.html"}
:::

**[[64.5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64.5 .sectionLabel}** A mortgage
administrator, mortgage broker or mortgage lender shall keep a large
virtual currency transaction record in respect of every amount of
\$10,000 or more in virtual currency that they receive in a single
transaction, unless the amount is received from a financial entity or
public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a client that is
a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 4](#1421265-1492459){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421265-1492459.html"}
:::

**[[64.6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-64.6 .sectionLabel}** A mortgage
administrator, mortgage broker or mortgage lender shall keep the
following records:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a receipt of funds record in respect of every amount
  that they receive in connection with a mortgage on real property or a
  hypothec on immovables, unless the amount is received from a financial
  entity or public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a
  client that is a financial entity or public body;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} an information record in respect of every person or
  entity

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a mortgage administrator, for which
    they service a mortgage agreement on real property or a hypothec
    agreement on immovables,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a mortgage broker, for which they
    arrange a loan secured by a mortgage on real property or by a
    hypothec on immovables, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a mortgage lender, to which they
    provide a loan secured by a mortgage on real property or by a
    hypothec on immovables or from which they raise funds for such a
    loan;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any loan secured by a mortgage on real
  property or by a hypothec on immovables that it enters into with a
  client, a record of the client's financial capacity, the terms of the
  loan, the nature of the client's principal business or their
  occupation and, if the client is a person, the name and address of
  their business or place of work; and

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the receipt of funds record or information record
  is in respect of a corporation, a copy of the part of official
  corporate records that contains any provision relating to the power to
  bind the corporation in respect of transactions with the mortgage
  administrator, mortgage broker or mortgage lender.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 4](#1421265-1492469){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421265-1492469.html"}
:::

### [Dealers in Precious Metals and Precious Stones]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295656 .Subheading}

- **[[65]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-65 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  dealer in precious metals and precious stones, other than a department
  or an agent of His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary
  of His Majesty in right of a province, that buys or sells precious
  metals, precious stones or jewellery, for an amount of \$10,000 or
  more is engaged in an activity for the purposes of paragraph 5(i) of
  the Act. A department or an agent of His Majesty in right of Canada or
  an agent or mandatary of His Majesty in right of a province carries
  out an activity for the purposes of paragraph 5(l) of the Act when
  they sell precious metals to the public for an amount of \$10,000 or
  more.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The activities referred to in subsection (1) do not
  include a purchase or sale that is carried out in the course of or in
  connection with manufacturing a product that contains precious metals
  or precious stones, extracting precious metals or precious stones from
  a mine or polishing or cutting precious stones.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the activities referred to in
  subsection (1) include the sale of precious metals, precious stones or
  jewellery that are left on consignment with a dealer in precious
  metals and precious stones. Goods left with an auctioneer for sale at
  auction are not considered to be left on consignment.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295662){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179465-1295662.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 18(E)](#1421305-1492543){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421305-1492543.html"}
:::

Version](section-65-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 65"){.PITLink}
:::
