### [Casinos]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295682 .Subheading}

- **[[70]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-70 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  casino shall report the following transactions and information to the
  Centre:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the receipt from a person or entity of an amount of
    \$10,000 or more in cash in a single transaction, together with the
    information set out in Schedule 1;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the initiation, at the request of a person or
    entity, of an international electronic funds transfer of \$10,000 or
    more in a single transaction, together with the information set out
    in Schedule 2;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the final receipt of an international electronic
    funds transfer of \$10,000 or more in a single transaction, together
    with the information set out in Schedule 3; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the receipt from a person or entity of an amount of
    \$10,000 or more in virtual currency in a single transaction,
    together with the information set out in Schedule 4.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A casino is not required to report the transaction
  and information under paragraph (1)(a) if the amount is received from
  a financial entity or public body or from a person who is acting on
  behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295691){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179465-1295691.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-70-20060322.html "Link to previous version of section 70"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[71]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-71 .sectionLabel}** A casino shall report to
the Centre the disbursement, in any of the following transactions, of
\$10,000 or more in a single transaction, together with the information
set out in Schedule 6:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the redemption of chips, tokens or plaques;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a front cash withdrawal;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} a safekeeping withdrawal;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} an advance on any form of credit, including an
  advance by a marker or a counter cheque;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} a payment on a bet, including a slot jackpot;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} a payment to a client of funds received for credit to
  that client or another client;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} the cashing of a cheque or the redemption of another
  negotiable instrument; and

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} a reimbursement to a client of travel or
  entertainment expenses.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295702){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179465-1295702.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-71-20170617.html "Link to previous version of section 71"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[72]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-72 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  casino shall keep a large cash transaction record in respect of every
  amount of \$10,000 or more in cash that the casino receives from a
  person or entity in a single transaction, unless the amount is
  received from a financial entity or public body or from a person who
  is acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public
  body.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the transactions referred to
  in subsection (1) include the following:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sale of chips, tokens or plaques;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a front cash deposit;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a safekeeping deposit;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the repayment of any form of credit, including
    repayment by a marker or a counter cheque;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a bet of fiat currency; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a sale of the casino's cheques.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295713){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179465-1295713.html"}
:::

**[[73]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-73 .sectionLabel}** A casino shall keep a
large virtual currency transaction record in respect of every amount of
\$10,000 or more in virtual currency that the casino receives from a
person or entity in a single transaction, unless the amount is received
from a financial entity or public body or from a person who is acting on
behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295716){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179465-1295716.html"}
:::

- **[[74]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-74 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  casino shall keep the following records in respect of every account
  that the casino opens:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} every signature card;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a record of the name, address and telephone number
    of each account holder and of every other person who is authorized
    to give instructions in respect of the account, the nature of their
    principal business or their occupation and, in the case of a person,
    their date of birth;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if an account holder is a corporation, a copy of
    the part of official corporate records that contains any provision
    relating to the power to bind the corporation in respect of the
    transaction or account;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} a record that sets out the intended use of the
    account;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} a record of every application in respect of the
    account;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} every account operating agreement that the casino
    creates or receives in respect of the account;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} a deposit slip in respect of every deposit that is
    made into the account; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} every debit and credit memo that the casino creates
    or receives in respect of the account.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A casino shall keep the following records in respect
  of every transaction that is conducted with it:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the casino extends credit of \$3,000 or more to
    a person or entity, a record of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name and address, the
      nature of their principal business or their occupation and, in the
      case of a person, their date of birth, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the terms of the extension of credit, the date
      on which credit was extended and the amount of credit extended;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a foreign currency exchange transaction ticket in
    respect of every foreign currency exchange transaction;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the casino initiates an electronic funds
    transfer of \$1,000 or more at the request of a person or entity, a
    record of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer
      is initiated,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that
      is involved in the initiation,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the person's or entity's name, address and
      telephone number, the nature of their principal business or their
      occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
      the initiation, the type of account and the name of each account
      holder,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
      electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
      an account number, and

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected
      by the electronic funds transfer, other than those referred to in
      subparagraph (vi);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the casino sends an international electronic
    funds transfer of \$1,000 or more that was initiated by another
    person or entity, a record of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer
      is sent,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} if the casino exchanges fiat currencies in the
      course of sending the electronic funds transfer, the type and
      amount of each fiat currency that is involved in the exchange,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used and their source,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
      the sending, the type of account and the name of each account
      holder, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
      sending and has a function equivalent to that of an account
      number,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the name, address and telephone number of the
      person or entity that requested the initiation of the electronic
      funds transfer, unless that information was not, despite the
      taking of reasonable measures, included with the transfer and is
      not otherwise known, and

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of each beneficiary,
      unless that information was not, despite the taking of reasonable
      measures, included with the transfer and is not otherwise known;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the casino is the final recipient of an
    international electronic funds transfer of \$1,000 or more, a record
    of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the date on which the electronic funds transfer
      is finally received,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the type and amount of each type of funds that
      is involved in the final receipt,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name, address and telephone number of each
      beneficiary, the nature of their principal business or their
      occupation and, in the case of a person, their date of birth,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the date of the remittance,

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the exchange rates used for the remittance and
      their source,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is in funds, the type and
      amount of each type of funds involved,

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} if the remittance is not in funds, the type of
      remittance and its value, if different from the amount of funds
      finally received,

    - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected
      by the final receipt or the remittance, the type of account and
      the name of each account holder,

    - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} every reference number that is connected to the
      electronic funds transfer and has a function equivalent to that of
      an account number,

    - [(x)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the person or entity that
      requested the initiation of the electronic funds transfer, unless
      that information was not, despite the taking of reasonable
      measures, included with the transfer and is not otherwise known,
      and

    - [(xi)]{.lawlabel} the number of every account that is affected by
      the electronic funds transfer, other than those referred to in
      subparagraph (viii); and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the casino receives an amount of \$3,000 or more
    from a person or entity, a receipt of funds record in respect of
    that amount, unless that amount is received from a financial entity
    or public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a client
    that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295763){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179465-1295763.html"}
- [SOR/2020-112, s. 7](#1248018-1295764){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1248018-1295764.html"}
- [SOR/2023-193, s. 11](#1421154-1485968){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421154-1485968.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-74-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 74"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[75]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-75 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
sections 70 to 74, if a [[registered charity]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
defined in subsection 248(1) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3),
conducts and manages, in a permanent establishment of a casino, for a
period of not more than two consecutive days at a time, a lottery scheme
that includes games of roulette or card games and, in doing so, acts
under the supervision of the government of a province that is referred
to in paragraph 5(k) of the Act, or of an organization that is referred
to in paragraph 5(k.2) of the Act, that conducts and manages such a
lottery scheme in the same establishment, the lottery scheme that is
conducted and managed by the registered charity is considered to be
conducted and managed by the supervising government or organization.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295767){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179465-1295767.html"}
:::

### [Departments and Agents or Mandataries of His Majesty in Right of Canada or of a Province]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295768 .Subheading}

::: HistoricalNote
\[

- SOR/2023-194, s. 18(E)

\]
:::

**[[76]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-76 .sectionLabel}** A department or an agent
of His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His
Majesty in right of a province carries out an activity for the purposes
of paragraph 5(l) of the Act when they accept deposit liabilities in the
course of providing financial services to the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295771){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179465-1295771.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 18(E)](#1421305-1492544){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421305-1492544.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-76-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 76"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[77]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-77 .sectionLabel}** A department or an agent
of His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His
Majesty in right of a province carries out an activity for the purposes
of paragraph 5(l) of the Act when they issue, sell or redeem money
orders in the course of providing financial services to the public.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 36](#1179465-1295774){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179465-1295774.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 18(E)](#1421305-1492545){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421305-1492545.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-77-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 77"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[78]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-78 .sectionLabel}** A department or an agent
of His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His
Majesty in right of a province that receives an amount of \$10,000 or
more in cash in a single transaction in connection with an activity
referred to in section 77 shall report the transaction to the Centre,
together with the information set out in Schedule 1, unless the amount
is received from a financial entity or public body or from a person who
is acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public
body.

::: HistoricalNote

  ajax-after="1179468-1295823.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 18(E)](#1421305-1492550){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421305-1492550.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-78-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 78"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[79]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-79 .sectionLabel}** A department or an agent
of His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His
Majesty in right of a province that receives an amount of \$10,000 or
more in virtual currency in a single transaction in connection with an
activity referred to in section 77 shall report the transaction to the
Centre, together with the information set out in Schedule 4.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 38](#1179468-1295826){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179468-1295826.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 18(E)](#1421305-1492546){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421305-1492546.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-79-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 79"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[80]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-80 .sectionLabel}** A department or an agent
of His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His
Majesty in right of a province shall keep a large cash transaction
record in respect of every amount of \$10,000 or more in cash that they
receive in a single transaction in connection with an activity referred
to in section 77, unless the amount is received from a financial entity
or public body or from a person who is acting on behalf of a client that
is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 38](#1179468-1295829){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179468-1295829.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 18(E)](#1421305-1492547){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421305-1492547.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-80-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 80"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[81]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-81 .sectionLabel}** A department or an agent
of His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His
Majesty in right of a province shall keep a large virtual currency
transaction record in respect of every amount of \$10,000 or more in
virtual currency that they receive in a single transaction in connection
with an activity referred to in section 77, unless the amount is
received from a financial entity or public body or from a person who is
acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity or public body.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 38](#1179468-1295832){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179468-1295832.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 18(E)](#1421305-1492551){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421305-1492551.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-81-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 81"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[82]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-82 .sectionLabel}** A department or an agent
of His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His
Majesty in right of a province shall keep the following records in
connection with an activity referred to in section 77:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if they receive an amount of \$3,000 or more as
  consideration for the issuance or sale of one or more money orders
  from a person or entity other than either a financial entity or a
  person who is acting on behalf of a client that is a financial entity,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a record of the date of the receipt,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a person, a record of their name,
    address, date of birth and the nature of their principal business or
    their occupation,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity, an information record,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a record of the amount received,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a record indicating whether the amount received is
    in funds or virtual currency and the type and amount of each type of
    funds and each of the virtual currencies involved,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a record of the number of every account that is
    affected by the transaction, the type of account and the name of
    each account holder,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a record of every reference number that is
    connected to the transaction and has a function equivalent to that
    of an account number, and

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} if the amount received is in virtual currency,
    every transaction identifier, including the sending and receiving
    addresses;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if they redeem one or more money orders that total
  \$3,000 or more at the request of a person or entity,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a record of the date of the redemption,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a person, a record of their name,
    address, date of birth and the nature of their principal business or
    their occupation,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity, an information record,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a record of the total amount of the money order or
    orders,

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a record of the name of the issuer of each money
    order,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} a record of the number of every account that is
    affected by the redemption, the type of account and the name of each
    account holder,

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} a record of every reference number that is
    connected to the redemption and has a function equivalent to that of
    an account number; and

  - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} if the redemption involves virtual currency,
    every transaction identifier, including the sending and receiving
    addresses; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the information record is in respect of a
  corporation, a copy of the part of official corporate records that
  contains any provision relating to the power to bind the corporation
  in respect of transactions with the department or the agent or
  mandatary.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 38](#1179468-1295854){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179468-1295854.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 18(E)](#1421305-1492548){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421305-1492548.html"}
:::

Version](section-82-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 82"){.PITLink}
:::

## [PART 2]{.HLabel1}[Requirement To Verify Identity]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1295855 .Part}

### [Application of Parts 5 and 6]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295856 .Subheading}

**[[83]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-83 .sectionLabel}** The provisions of this
Part are subject to the provisions of Parts 5 and 6.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 38](#1179468-1295859){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179468-1295859.html"}
:::

### [Persons and Entities Required To Keep Large Cash Transaction Record or Large Virtual Currency Transaction Record]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295860 .Subheading}

**[[84]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-84 .sectionLabel}** A person or entity shall
verify, in accordance with section 105, 109 or 112, the identity of a
person or entity from which they receive an amount

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which they are required to keep a large
  cash transaction record under these Regulations, unless the amount is
  received as a deposit made into a business account or a deposit made
  by means of an automated banking machine; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which they are required to keep a large
  virtual currency transaction record under these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 38](#1179468-1295865){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179468-1295865.html"}
:::

### [Suspicious Transactions]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295866 .Subheading}

- **[[85]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-85 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person or entity that is subject to these Regulations shall take
  reasonable measures to verify, in accordance with section 105, 109 or
  112, the identity of a person or entity that conducts or attempts to
  conduct a transaction with them that is required to be reported to the
  Centre under section 7 of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the person or entity believes that taking the
  reasonable measures would inform the person or entity that conducts or
  attempts to conduct a transaction with them that the transaction and
  related information will be reported under section 7 of the Act, they
  are not required to comply with subsection (1).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 38](#1179468-1295871){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179468-1295871.html"}
:::

### [Financial Entities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295872 .Subheading}

**[[86]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-86 .sectionLabel}** A financial entity shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person for whom it opens an account, other than a
    credit card account or a prepaid payment product account,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a person, other than an account holder, who is
    authorized to give instructions in respect of an account, other than
    a credit card account or a prepaid payment product account, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a person -- other than one referred to in
    subparagraph (i) or (ii) --- who

    - [(A)]{.lawlabel} requests that it issue or redeem money orders,
      traveller's cheques or similar negotiable instruments in an amount
      of \$3,000 or more,

    - [(B)]{.lawlabel} requests that it initiate an international
      electronic funds transfer --- or any other electronic funds
      transfer that is a SWIFT MT-103 message or its equivalent --- of
      \$1,000 or more,

    - [(C)]{.lawlabel} requests that it exchange an amount of \$3,000 or
      more in a foreign currency exchange transaction,

    - [(D)]{.lawlabel} requests that it transfer an amount of \$1,000 or
      more in virtual currency,

    - [(E)]{.lawlabel} requests that it exchange an amount of \$1,000 or
      more in a virtual currency exchange transaction, or

    - [(F)]{.lawlabel} is the beneficiary of an international electronic
      funds transfer of \$1,000 or more, or of a transfer of an amount
      of \$1,000 or more in virtual currency, to whom it makes the
      remittance;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation for which it opens an account, other than a credit
  card account or a prepaid payment product account; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of an entity, other than a corporation, for which it opens an account,
  other than a credit card account or a prepaid payment product account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 38](#1179468-1295887){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179468-1295887.html"}
- [SOR/2020-112, s. 8](#1248020-1295888){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1248020-1295888.html"}
:::

**[[87]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-87 .sectionLabel}** A financial entity shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of a person for whom it opens a credit card account;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation for which it opens a credit card account; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of an entity, other than a corporation, for which it opens a credit
  card account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 40](#1179472-1295908){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179472-1295908.html"}
:::

**[[88]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-88 .sectionLabel}** A financial entity shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a person for whom it opens a prepaid payment
    product account,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an authorized user, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} any other person who makes a payment of \$1,000
    or more to a prepaid payment product account;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a corporation for which it opens a prepaid payment
    product account, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any other corporation that makes a payment of
    \$1,000 or more to a prepaid payment product account; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an entity, other than a corporation, for which it
    opens a prepaid payment product account, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} any other entity, other than a corporation, that
    makes a payment of \$1,000 or more to a prepaid payment product
    account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 40](#1179472-1295921){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179472-1295921.html"}
:::

**[[89]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-89 .sectionLabel}** A trust company shall
also

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of a person who is the settlor of an [inter vivos]{.otherLang
  lang="la"} trust in respect of which it is required to keep a record
  under subsection 15(1);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation that is the settlor of an institutional trust in
  respect of which it is required to keep a record under subsection
  15(1);

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of an entity, other than a corporation, that is the settlor of an
  institutional trust in respect of which it is required to keep a
  record under subsection 15(1);

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of a person who is authorized to act as a co-trustee of a trust; and

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if an entity is authorized to act as a co-trustee of
  a trust,

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
    of all persons --- up to three --- who are authorized to give
    instructions with respect to the entity's activities as co-trustee,
    and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109 or 112, verify the
    identity of the entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 40](#1179472-1295931){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179472-1295931.html"}
:::

**[[90]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-90 .sectionLabel}** A financial entity that
enters into a correspondent banking relationship

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} shall ascertain the name and address of the foreign
  financial institution by examining a copy of the foreign financial
  institution's banking licence, banking charter, authorization or
  certification to operate issued by the competent authority under the
  legislation of the jurisdiction in which the foreign financial
  institution was incorporated, of its certificate of incorporation or
  of a similar document;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} shall take reasonable measures to

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ascertain, based on information that is accessible
    to the public, whether any civil or criminal penalties have been
    imposed on the foreign financial institution in respect of
    anti-money laundering or anti-terrorist financing requirements and,
    if such a penalty has been imposed, shall monitor all transactions
    conducted in the context of the correspondent banking relationship
    to detect any transactions that are required to be reported to the
    Centre under section 7 of the Act,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} assess, based on information that is accessible to
    the public, the reputation of the foreign financial institution with
    respect to its compliance with anti-money laundering and
    anti-terrorist financing requirements, and

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} assess, based on information that is accessible
    to the public, the quality of the anti-money laundering and
    anti-terrorist financing supervision of the jurisdiction in which
    the foreign financial institution was incorporated and the
    jurisdiction in which it conducts transactions in the context of the
    correspondent banking relationship; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} shall take reasonable measures to determine the
  nature of the clientele and markets served by the foreign financial
  institution.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 40](#1179472-1295936){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179472-1295936.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 5](#1421267-1492476){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421267-1492476.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-90-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 90"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[91]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-91 .sectionLabel}** If a client of a foreign
financial institution has direct access to services provided by the
financial entity under a correspondent banking relationship, the
financial entity shall take reasonable measures to ascertain whether the
foreign financial institution

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} has, in respect of its clients that have direct
  access to the accounts of the financial entity, met requirements that
  are consistent with the requirements of section 86; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} has agreed to provide relevant client identification
  information on request to the financial entity.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 40](#1179472-1295941){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179472-1295941.html"}
:::

### [Life Insurance Companies and Life Insurance Brokers or Agents]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295942 .Subheading}

**[[92]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-92 .sectionLabel}** A life insurance company
or life insurance broker or agent shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of a person in respect of whom they are required to keep an
  information record under section 22;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation in respect of which they are required to keep an
  information record under section 22; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of an entity, other than a corporation, in respect of which they are
  required to keep an information record under section 22.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 40](#1179472-1295957){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179472-1295957.html"}
:::

**[[93]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93 .sectionLabel}** Section 92 does not apply
to a life insurance company or a life insurance broker or agent when
they are dealing in reinsurance.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 40](#1179472-1295960){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179472-1295960.html"}
:::

### [Factors]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1526974 .Subheading}

- **[[93.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93.1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  factor shall verify,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, the identity of a
    person in respect of whom it is required to keep a record under
    section 24.14;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, the identity of a
    corporation in respect of which it is required to keep a record
    under section 24.14; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, the identity of an
    entity, other than a corporation, in respect of which it is required
    to keep a record under section 24.14.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(b) and (c) do not apply if the entity
  is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a public body;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a corporation or trust that has minimum net assets
    of \$75 million on its last audited balance sheet, whose shares or
    units are traded on a Canadian stock exchange or a stock exchange
    designated under subsection 262(1) of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) and that operates in a country that is a
    member of the Financial Action Task Force; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a subsidiary of a public body referred to in
    paragraph (a) or a corporation or trust referred to in paragraph
    (b), whose financial statements are consolidated with the financial
    statements of the public body, corporation or trust.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 6](#1517548-1526985){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517548-1526985.html"}
:::

### [Financing or Leasing Entity]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1526986 .Subheading}

- **[[93.2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-93.2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  financing or leasing entity shall verify

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, the identity of a
    person in respect of whom it is required to keep a record under
    section 24.2;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, the identity of a
    corporation in respect of which it is required to keep a record
    under section 24.2; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, the identity of an
    entity, other than a corporation, in respect of which it is required
    to keep a record under section 24.2.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(b) and (c) do not apply if the entity
  is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a public body;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a corporation or trust that has minimum net assets
    of \$75 million on its last audited balance sheet, whose shares or
    units are traded on a Canadian stock exchange or a stock exchange
    designated under subsection 262(1) of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) and that operates in a country that is a
    member of the Financial Action Task Force; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a subsidiary of a public body referred to in
    paragraph (a) or a corporation or trust referred to in paragraph
    (b), whose financial statements are consolidated with the financial
    statements of the public body, corporation or trust.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 6](#1517548-1526997){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517548-1526997.html"}
:::

### [Securities Dealers]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295961 .Subheading}

**[[94]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-94 .sectionLabel}** A securities dealer shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of a person for whom they open an account and every other person who
  is authorized to give instructions in respect of the account;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation for which they open an account; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of an entity, other than a corporation, for which they open an
  account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 40](#1179472-1295967){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179472-1295967.html"}
:::

### [Money Services Businesses and Foreign Money Services Businesses]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295968 .Subheading}

- **[[95]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-95 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  money services business --- or a foreign money services business in
  connection with services that they provide in Canada --- shall, in
  accordance with section 105, verify the identity of a person who

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} requests that they issue or redeem money orders,
    traveller's cheques or similar negotiable instruments in an amount
    of \$3,000 or more;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} requests that they transmit an amount of \$1,000
    or more in funds, other than in the case of an electronic funds
    transfer;

  - [(a.2)]{.lawlabel} requests that they cash one or more cheques that
    total \$3,000 or more;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} requests that they initiate an electronic funds
    transfer of \$1,000 or more;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} requests that they exchange an amount of \$3,000 or
    more in a foreign currency exchange transaction;

  - [(c.1)]{.lawlabel} requests that they transport an amount of \$1,000
    or more in cash or [[virtual currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within
    the meaning of paragraph (b) of the definition of that term in
    subsection 1(2) or an amount of \$3,000 or more in money orders,
    traveller's cheques or other similar negotiable instruments;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} requests that they transfer an amount of \$1,000 or
    more in virtual currency;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} requests that they exchange an amount of \$1,000 or
    more in a virtual currency exchange transaction;

  - [(e.1)]{.lawlabel} is a beneficiary of an amount of \$1,000 or more
    in funds, to whom they make the remittance, other than in the case
    of an electronic funds transfer;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} is a beneficiary of an international electronic
    funds transfer of \$1,000 or more, or of a transfer of an amount of
    \$1,000 or more in virtual currency, to whom they make the
    remittance;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} is a person in respect of whom they are required to
    keep an information record under paragraph 36.1(a); or

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} donates an amount of \$1,000 or more in funds or
    virtual currency using a crowdfunding platform that is provided and
    maintained by the money services business or foreign money services
    business.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs (1)(a) to (e) do not apply if an employee
  who is authorized to order transactions under an agreement referred to
  in section 37 conducts the transaction on behalf of their employer
  under the agreement.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A money services business --- or foreign money
  services business in connection with services that they provide in
  Canada --- shall, in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that requests that they transport an amount of
    \$1,000 or more in cash or [[virtual
    currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within the meaning of paragraph (b)
    of the definition of that term in subsection 1(2) or an amount of
    \$3,000 or more in money orders, traveller's cheques or other
    similar negotiable instruments;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which they are required to keep an
    information record under paragraph 36.1(a);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which they are required to keep an
    information record under section 37;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that donates an amount of \$1,000 or more in funds
    or virtual currency using a crowdfunding platform that is provided
    and maintained by the money services business or foreign money
    services business; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that requests that they cash one or more cheques
    that total \$3,000 or more.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} A money services business --- or foreign money
  services business in connection with services that they provide in
  Canada --- shall, in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of an entity, other than a corporation,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} that requests that they transport an amount of
    \$1,000 or more in cash or [[virtual
    currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} within the meaning of paragraph (b)
    of the definition of that term in subsection 1(2) or an amount of
    \$3,000 or more in money orders, traveller's cheques or other
    similar negotiable instruments;

  - [(a.1)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which they are required to keep an
    information record under paragraph 36.1(a);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of which they are required to keep an
    information record under section 37;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} that donates an amount of \$1,000 or more in funds
    or virtual currency using a crowdfunding platform that is provided
    and maintained by the money services business or foreign money
    services business; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} that requests that they cash one or more cheques
    that total \$3,000 or more.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (3) and (4) do not apply if the entity is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a public body;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a corporation or trust that has minimum net assets
    of \$75 million on its last audited balance sheet, whose shares or
    units are traded on a Canadian stock exchange or a stock exchange
    designated under subsection 262(1) of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) and that operates in a country that is a
    member of the Financial Action Task Force; or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a subsidiary of a public body referred to in
    paragraph (a) or a corporation or trust referred to in paragraph
    (b), whose financial statements are consolidated with the financial
    statements of the public body, corporation or trust.

### [British Columbia Notaries Public and British Columbia Notary Corporations]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295989 .Subheading}

**[[96]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-96 .sectionLabel}** A British Columbia notary
public or British Columbia notary corporation shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of a person who conducts a transaction in respect of which they are
  required to keep a record under section 43;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation that conducts a transaction referred to in
  paragraph (a) or on whose behalf a person conducts such a transaction;
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of an entity, other than a corporation, that conducts a transaction
  referred to in paragraph (a) or on whose behalf a person conducts such
  a transaction.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 40](#1179472-1295995){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179472-1295995.html"}
:::

### [Legal Counsel and Legal Firms]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1295779 .Subheading}

- **[[97]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-97
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (2) and 62(2)
  and section 63, every legal counsel and every legal firm shall, in
  respect of a transaction for which a record is required to be kept
  under section 33.4,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with subsection 64(1), ascertain the
    identity of every person who conducts the transaction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 65, confirm the
    existence of and ascertain the name and address of every corporation
    on whose behalf the transaction is conducted and the names of the
    corporation's directors; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 66, confirm the
    existence of every entity, other than a corporation, on whose behalf
    the transaction is conducted.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a
  transaction for which funds are received by a legal counsel or legal
  firm from the trust account of a legal firm or from the trust account
  of a legal counsel who is not acting on behalf of their employer.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2007-293, s. 23
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 37](#1179467-1297572){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179467-1297572.html"}
:::

**[[98]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-98 .sectionLabel}** Any legal counsel or
legal firm that is required to ascertain the identity of any person or
confirm the existence of any entity in accordance with section 59.4
shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} conduct ongoing monitoring of its business
  relationship with that person or entity; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} keep a record of the measures taken and the
  information obtained under paragraph (a).

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-15, s. 13
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 37](#1179467-1297559){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179467-1297559.html"}
:::

**[[99]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-99 .sectionLabel}** If, as a result of its
ongoing monitoring of a business relationship under paragraph 59.41(a),
the legal counsel or legal firm considers that the risk of a money
laundering offence or terrorist activity financing offence is high, it
shall treat that person or entity as high risk for the purpose of
subsection 9.6(3) of the Act and apply the prescribed special measures
in accordance with section 71.1 of these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- SOR/2013-15, s. 13
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 37](#1179467-1297563){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179467-1297563.html"}
:::

### [Accountants and Accounting Firms]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1296000 .Subheading}

**[[100]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-100 .sectionLabel}** An accountant or
accounting firm shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of a person who conducts a transaction in respect of which they are
  required to keep a record under section 52;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation that conducts a transaction referred to in
  paragraph (a) or on whose behalf a person conducts such a transaction;
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of an entity, other than a corporation, that conducts a transaction
  referred to in paragraph (a) or on whose behalf a person conducts such
  a transaction.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 44](#1179479-1296012){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179479-1296012.html"}
:::

### [Real Estate Brokers or Sales Representatives]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1296013 .Subheading}

- **[[101]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-101 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  real estate broker or sales representative shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
    of a person who conducts a transaction in respect of which they are
    required to keep a record under subsection 58(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
    of a corporation that conducts a transaction referred to in
    paragraph (a) or on whose behalf a person conducts such a
    transaction; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
    of an entity, other than a corporation, that conducts a transaction
    referred to in paragraph (a) or on whose behalf a person conducts
    such a transaction.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If every party to a transaction is represented by a
  real estate broker or sales representative, each real estate broker or
  sales representative is required only to verify the identity of the
  party or parties that they represent.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If one or more but not all of the parties to a
  transaction are represented by a real estate broker or sales
  representative, each real estate broker or sales representative shall
  take reasonable measures to verify the identity of the party or
  parties that are not represented.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a real estate broker or sales representative is
  unable to verify the identity of a party under subsection (3), they
  shall keep a record that sets out the measures taken, the date on
  which each measure was taken and the reasons why the measures were
  unsuccessful.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 44](#1179479-1296023){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179479-1296023.html"}
:::

**[[102]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102 .sectionLabel}** A real estate developer
shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity

  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity

### [Mortgage Administrators, Mortgage Brokers and Mortgage Lenders]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1492477 .Subheading}

**[[102.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-102.1 .sectionLabel}** A mortgage
administrator, mortgage broker or mortgage lender shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of a person who conducts a transaction in respect of which they are
  required to keep a record under section 64.6;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation that conducts a transaction referred to in
  paragraph (a) or on whose behalf a person conducts such a transaction;
  and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity

### [Casinos]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1296031 .Subheading}

**[[103]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-103 .sectionLabel}** A casino shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of a person

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} for whom the casino opens an account,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} who is authorized to give instructions in respect
    of the account but who does not hold an account with the casino,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} who receives a disbursement in respect of which
    the casino is required to make a report under section 71,

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} who conducts a transaction in respect of which the
    casino is required to keep a record under paragraph 74(2)(a) or (f),

  - [(v)]{.lawlabel} who requests that the casino exchange an amount of
    \$3,000 or more in a foreign currency exchange transaction,

  - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} who requests that the casino initiate an
    electronic funds transfer in respect of which the casino is required
    to keep a record under paragraph 74(2)(c), or

  - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} who is the beneficiary of an international
    electronic funds transfer in respect of which the casino is required
    to keep a record under paragraph 74(2)(e);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation for which the casino opens an account; and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of an entity, other than a corporation, for which the casino opens an
  account.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 44](#1179479-1296044){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179479-1296044.html"}
- [SOR/2020-112, s. 10](#1248031-1296045){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1248031-1296045.html"}
:::

**[[104]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-104 .sectionLabel}** A department or an
agent of His Majesty in right of Canada or an agent or mandatary of His
Majesty in right of a province shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the identity
  of a person in respect of whom they are required to keep a record
  under subparagraph 82(a)(ii) or (b)(ii);

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the identity
  of a corporation in respect of which they are required to keep an
  information record under subparagraph 82(a)(iii) or (b)(iii); and

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the identity
  of an entity, other than a corporation, in respect of which they are
  required to keep an information record under subparagraph 82(a)(iii)
  or (b)(iii).

## [PART 3]{.HLabel1}[Measures for Verifying Identity]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1296053 .Part}

- **[[105]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-105 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person or entity that is required to verify a person's identity shall
  do so

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} by referring to an identification document that
    contains the person's name and photograph and that is issued by the
    federal government or a provincial government or by a foreign
    government that is not a municipal government, and by confirming
    that the name and photograph are those of the person;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} by referring to information concerning the person
    that the person or entity that is verifying their identity receives,
    on request, from a federal or provincial government body --- or an
    agent or mandatary of that body --- that is authorized in Canada to
    verify the identity of persons, and by confirming that either the
    name and address or the name and date of birth included in the
    information are those of the person;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} by referring to information that is in the person's
    credit file --- if that file is located in Canada and has been in
    existence for at least three years and the information is derived
    from more than one source --- and by confirming that the name,
    address and date of birth in the credit file are those of the
    person;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} by doing any two of the following:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} referring to information from a reliable source
      that includes the person's name and address, and confirming that
      the name and address are those of the person,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} referring to information from a reliable source
      that includes the person's name and date of birth, and confirming
      that the name and date of birth are those of the person,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} referring to information that includes the
      person's name and confirms that they hold a deposit account, a
      prepaid payment product account or a credit card or other loan
      account with a financial entity, and confirming that information;
      or

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} by confirming that one of the following entities
    previously verified the person's identity in accordance with any of
    paragraphs (a) to (d) or previously ascertained the person's
    identity in accordance with these Regulations, as they read at the
    time, and that the name, address and date of birth in the entity's
    record are those of the person:

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an entity that is referred to in any of
      paragraphs 5(a) to (g) of the Act and that is affiliated with the
      entity that is verifying the person's identity,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an entity that carries out activities outside
      Canada that are similar to the activities of a person or entity
      referred to in any of paragraphs 5(a) to (g) of the Act and that
      is affiliated with the entity that is verifying the person's
      identity,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a financial entity that is subject to the Act
      and that is a member of the same financial services cooperative or
      credit union central as the entity that is verifying the person's
      identity.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The identity of a person who is under 12 years of age
  shall be verified for the purposes of subsection (1) by verifying the
  identity of one of their parents or their guardian or tutor.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The identity of a person who is at least 12 years of
  age but not more than 15 years of age may be verified by referring
  under subparagraph (1)(d)(i) to information that includes the name and
  address of one of the person's parents or their guardian or tutor, and
  by confirming that the address is that of the person.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subparagraphs (1)(d)(i) to (iii),
  the information that is referred to must not be from, or derived from,
  the same source and neither the person whose identity is being
  verified nor the person or entity that is verifying their identity can
  be a source. If information in a credit file is referred to, the
  credit file must have been in existence for at least six months.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} A document that is used by a person or entity to
  verify identity under subsection (1) must be authentic, valid and
  current. Other information that is used for that purpose must be valid
  and current.

- [(6)]{.lawlabel} In the case of a retail deposit account referred to
  in subsection 627.17(1) of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01), if a
  person or entity cannot verify a person's identity in accordance with
  one of paragraphs (1)(a) to (e) of this section, they are deemed to
  comply with subsection (1) if the person who requests that the account
  be opened meets the conditions set out in subsections 627.17(1)
  and (3) of the [Bank Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01).

- [(7)]{.lawlabel} The identity shall be verified

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in section 84,
    subparagraphs 86(a)(iii) and 88(a)(iii), paragraphs 95(1)(a) to (f),
    96(a), 97(1)(a), 100(a), 101(1)(a), 102(a) and 102.1(a),
    subparagraphs 103(a)(iii) to (vii) and paragraph 104(a), at the time
    of the transaction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraph (j), in the cases referred to
    in subparagraphs 86(a)(i) and (ii) and paragraph 94(a), before the
    first transaction, other than an initial deposit, is carried out on
    the account;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in section 85, before the
    transaction or attempted transaction is reported under section 7 of
    the Act;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 87(a), before
    any credit card issued on the account is activated;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in subparagraph 88(a)(i),
    before the prepaid payment product account is activated;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in subparagraph 88(a)(ii),
    before the first transaction is carried out by the authorized user
    on the prepaid payment product account;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraphs 89(a)
    and (d) and subparagraph 89(e)(i), within 15 days after the day on
    which the trust company becomes the trustee;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} subject to paragraph (j), in the case referred to
    in paragraph 92(a), within 30 days after the day on which the
    information record is created;

  - [(h.001)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraphs
    93.1(1)(a) and 93.2(1)(a), at the time the record is created;

  - [(h.01)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 95(1)(c.1),
    before the first transport of cash, virtual currency or negotiable
    instruments is carried out;

  - [(h.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 95(1)(g), at
    the time the information record is created;

  - [(h.2)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 95(1)(h), at
    the time of the donation;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in subparagraphs 103(a)(i)
    and (ii), before any funds are disbursed; and

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a group plan account, at the time a
    contribution in respect of a member of the group plan is made to the
    plan.

- **[[106]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-106 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person or entity that is required to verify a person's identity in
  accordance with subsection 105(1) may rely on an agent or mandatary to
  take the measures to do so.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person or entity may rely on measures that were
  previously taken by an agent or mandatary to verify the person's
  identity if the agent or mandatary was, at the time they took the
  measures,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acting in their own capacity, whether or not they
    were required to take the measures under these Regulations; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acting as an agent or mandatary under a written
    agreement or arrangement that was entered into, with another person
    or entity that is required to verify a person's identity, for the
    purposes of verifying identity in accordance with subsection 105(1)
    or, if the measures were taken before the coming into force of this
    subsection, that was entered into, with another person or entity
    that was required to ascertain a person's identity, for the purposes
    of ascertaining identity in accordance with these Regulations as
    they read at the time the measures were taken.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In order to rely, under subsection (1) or (2), on
  measures taken by an agent or mandatary, the person or entity shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have entered into a written agreement or
    arrangement with the agent or mandatary for the purposes of
    verifying a person's identity in accordance with subsection 105(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as soon as feasible, obtain from the agent or
    mandatary the information that the agent or mandatary referred to in
    order to verify the person's identity and the information that the
    agent or mandatary confirmed as being that of the person; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be satisfied that the information that the agent or
    mandatary confirmed as being that of the person is valid and current
    and that the agent or mandatary verified the person's identity in
    the manner described in one of paragraphs 105(1)(a) to (d) or, if
    the measures were taken before the coming into force of this
    section, that the agent or mandatary ascertained the person's
    identity in accordance with these Regulations, as they read at the
    time the measures were taken.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 44](#1179479-1296096){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179479-1296096.html"}
:::

- **[[107]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-107 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person or entity that is required to verify a person's identity in
  accordance with subsection 105(1) may rely on measures that were
  previously taken by another person or entity if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the other person or entity is referred to in
    section 5 of the Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the other entity is affiliated with the entity that
    is required to verify the person's identity or with another entity
    referred to in section 5 of the Act and carries out activities
    outside Canada that are similar to those of a person or entity that
    is referred to in any of paragraphs 5(a) to (g) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person or entity that is required to verify a
  person's identity shall not rely on measures that were previously
  taken by an entity referred to in paragraph (1)(b) unless they are
  satisfied, after taking into account the risk of money laundering or
  terrorist activity financing offences in the foreign state in which
  that entity carries out the activities, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the entity applies policies that establish
    requirements similar to those in sections 6, 6.1 and 9.6 of the Act;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the entity's compliance with those policies is
    subject to the supervision of a competent authority under the
    legislation of that foreign state.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In order to rely, under subsection (1), on measures
  taken by another person or entity, the person or entity that is
  required to verify a person's identity shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as soon as feasible, obtain from the other person
    or entity the information that was confirmed as being that of the
    person and be satisfied that the information is valid and current
    and that the other person or entity verified the person's identity
    in the manner described in one of paragraphs 105(1)(a) to (d) or, if
    the measures were taken before the coming into force of this
    section, that the other person or entity ascertained the person's
    identity in accordance with these Regulations, as they read at the
    time the measures were taken; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have a written agreement or arrangement with the
    other person or entity that requires the other person or entity to
    provide them on request, as soon as feasible, with all of the
    information that the other person or entity referred to in order to
    verify the person's identity.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 44](#1179479-1296108){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179479-1296108.html"}
:::

**[[108]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-108 .sectionLabel}** A person or entity that
is required to verify a person's identity under these Regulations shall
keep a record that sets out the person's name and the following
information:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the person or entity verified the person's
  identity in accordance with paragraph 105(1)(a), the date on which
  they did so, the type of document referred to, its number, the
  jurisdiction and country of issue of the document and, if applicable,
  its expiry date;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} if the person or entity verified the person's
  identity in accordance with paragraph 105(1)(b), the date on which
  they did so, the source of the information, the type of information
  referred to and a number associated with the information;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the person or entity verified the person's
  identity in accordance with paragraph 105(1)(c), the date on which
  they did so, the source of the information and the number of the
  person's credit file;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the person or entity verified the person's
  identity in accordance with paragraph 105(1)(d), the date on which
  they did so, the source of the information, the type of information
  referred to and the account number included in it or, if there is no
  account number included in it, a number associated with the
  information;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the entity verified the person's identity in
  accordance with paragraph 105(1)(e), the date on which it did so, the
  name of the entity that had previously verified that person's
  identity, the manner in which the person's identity had previously
  been verified under one of paragraphs 105(1)(a) to (d) and the
  applicable information referred to in one of paragraphs (a) to (d) of
  this section that is associated with that manner of verifying
  identity;

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the entity verified the person's identity in
  accordance with paragraph 105(1)(e) and the other entity had
  previously ascertained the person's identity before the coming into
  force of this section, the date on which the entity verified the
  person's identity in accordance with paragraph 105(1)(e), the name of
  the other entity, the manner in which the other entity had previously
  ascertained the person's identity in accordance with these
  Regulations, as they read at the time the other entity ascertained the
  person's identity, and the applicable information referred to in the
  record-keeping provision that is related to that manner, as it read at
  that time;

- [(g)]{.lawlabel} if, under subsection 105(6), the person or entity is
  deemed to have complied with subsection 105(1), the reasons why the
  person's identity could not be verified in the manner set out in one
  of paragraphs 105(1)(a) to (e) and the date on which the conditions
  set out in subsections 627.17(1) and (3) of the [Bank
  Act](/eng/acts/B-1.01) were met;

- [(h)]{.lawlabel} if, in accordance with subsection 106(1) or (2), the
  person or entity relied on measures taken by an agent or mandatary,
  all of the information that they obtain under paragraph 106(3)(b); or

- [(i)]{.lawlabel} if, in accordance with subsection 107(1), the person
  or entity relied on measures taken by another person or entity, all of
  the information that is provided to them under paragraph 107(3)(b).

::: HistoricalNote

- **[[109]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-109 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person or entity that is required to verify a corporation's identity
  shall do so by referring to its certificate of incorporation, to a
  record that it is required to file annually under applicable
  provincial securities legislation or to the most recent version of any
  other record that confirms its existence as a corporation and contains
  its name and address and the names of its directors.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A record that is used to verify identity under
  subsection (1) must be authentic, valid and current.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The names of a corporation's directors do not need to
  be confirmed if the corporation is a securities dealer.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} The corporation's identity shall be verified

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in section 84,
    subparagraph 88(b)(ii) and paragraph 95(3)(d), at the time of the
    transaction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in section 85, before the
    transaction or attempted transaction is reported under section 7 of
    the Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraphs 86(b) and
    103(b), before the first transaction, other than the initial
    deposit, is carried out on the account;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 87(b), before
    any credit card issued on the account is activated;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in subparagraph 88(b)(i),
    before the prepaid payment product account is activated;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraph 89(b) and
    subparagraph 89(e)(ii), within 15 days after the day on which the
    trust company becomes the trustee;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraphs 92(b),
    95(3)(b) and 104(b), within 30 days after the day on which the
    information record is created;

  - [(g.1)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraphs 93.1(1)(b)
    and 93.2(1)(b), at the time the record is created;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 94(b), within
    30 days after the day on which the account is opened;

  - [(h.01)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 95(3)(a),
    before the first transport of cash, virtual currency or negotiable
    instruments is carried out;

  - [(h.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 95(3)(a.1),
    at the time the information record is created;

  - [(h.2)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 95(3)(c), at
    the time of the donation; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraphs 96(b),
    97(1)(b), 100(b), 101(1)(b), 102(b) and 102.1(b), within 30 days
    after the day on which the transaction is conducted.

- [(5)]{.lawlabel} If a person or entity that is required to verify a
  corporation's identity does so by referring to an electronic version
  of a record that is contained in a database that is accessible to the
  public, they shall keep a record that sets out the corporation's
  registration number, the type of record referred to and the source of
  the electronic version of the record. In any other case, they shall
  keep the record or a copy of it.

- **[[110]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-110 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person or entity that is required to verify a corporation's identity
  in accordance with subsection 109(1) may rely on measures that were
  previously taken by another person or entity if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the other person or entity is referred to in
    section 5 of the Act; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the other entity is affiliated with the one that is
    required to verify the corporation's identity or with another entity
    referred to in section 5 of the Act and carries out activities
    outside Canada that are similar to those of a person or entity that
    is referred to in any of paragraphs 5(a) to (g) of the Act.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person or entity that is required to verify a
  corporation's identity shall not rely on measures that were previously
  taken by an entity referred to in paragraph (1)(b) unless they are
  satisfied, after taking into account the risk of money laundering or
  terrorist activity financing offences in the foreign state in which
  that entity carries out the activities, that

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the entity applies policies that establish
    requirements similar to those in sections 6, 6.1 and 9.6 of the Act;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the entity's compliance with those policies is
    subject to the supervision of a competent authority under the
    legislation of that foreign state.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} In order to rely, under subsection (1), on measures
  taken by another person or entity, the person or entity that is
  required to verify a corporation's identity shall

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} as soon as feasible, obtain from the other person
    or entity the information that was confirmed as being that of the
    corporation and be satisfied that the information is valid and
    current and that the other person or entity verified the
    corporation's identity in the manner described in subsection 109(1)
    or, if the measures were taken before the coming into force of this
    section, that the other person or entity confirmed the corporation's
    existence and ascertained its name and address and the names of its
    directors in accordance with these Regulations, as they read at the
    time the measures were taken; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} have a written agreement or arrangement with the
    other person or entity that requires the other person or entity to
    provide them on request, as soon as feasible, with all of the
    information that the other person or entity referred to in order to
    verify the corporation's identity.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 44](#1179479-1296167){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179479-1296167.html"}
:::

- **[[111]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-111 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person or entity that is referred to in any of paragraphs 5(a) to (g)
  of the Act and that is required to verify a corporation's identity in
  accordance with subsection 109(1) considers, based on an assessment of
  the risk referred to in subsection 9.6(2) of the Act, that there is a
  low risk of a money laundering offence or terrorist activity financing
  offence, they are deemed to comply with subsection 109(1) if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the corporation whose identity is to be verified

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is an entity that is referred to in any of those
      paragraphs 5(a) to (g),

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is a foreign corporation that carries out
      activities that are similar to those of an entity that is referred
      to in any of those paragraphs,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} administers a pension or investment fund that
      is regulated under the legislation of a foreign state and that
      either is created by a foreign government or is subject to the
      supervision of a competent authority under the legislation of that
      foreign state,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} is one whose shares are traded on a Canadian
      stock exchange or a stock exchange designated under subsection
      262(1) of the [Income Tax Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3),

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} is a subsidiary of a corporation that is referred
      to in any of subparagraphs (i) to (iv) or of an entity that is
      referred to in any of subparagraphs 114(1)(a)(i) to (iv) and is
      one whose financial statements are consolidated with the financial
      statements of that corporation or entity,

    - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} is an institution or agency of, or is owned by,
      the government of a foreign state, or

    - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} is a [[public service
      body]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as defined in subsection 123(1) of
      the [Excise Tax Act](/eng/acts/E-15);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} within the applicable time referred to in one of
    paragraphs 109(4)(a) to (i), they are satisfied that the corporation
    exists and that every person who deals with them on behalf of the
    corporation is authorized by it to do so; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} they keep a record that sets out the grounds for
    considering that there is a low risk of a money laundering offence
    or terrorist activity financing offence and the information obtained
    about the corporation and the persons referred to in paragraph (b).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the person or entity subsequently considers, based
  on an assessment of the risk referred to in subsection 9.6(2) of the
  Act, that the risk of a money laundering offence or terrorist activity
  financing offence has increased and is no longer low, they shall
  verify the corporation's identity in accordance with subsection 109(1)
  as soon as feasible.

- **[[112]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-112 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person or entity that is required to verify the identity of an entity
  other than a corporation shall do so by referring to a partnership
  agreement, to articles of association or to the most recent version of
  any other record that confirms its existence and contains its name and
  address.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} A record that is used to verify identity under
  subsection (1) must be authentic, valid and current.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} The entity's identity shall be verified

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in section 84,
    subparagraph 88(c)(ii) and paragraph 95(4)(d), at the time of the
    transaction;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in section 85, before the
    transaction or attempted transaction is reported under section 7 of
    the Act;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraphs 86(c) and
    103(c), before the first transaction, other than the initial
    deposit, is carried out on the account;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 87(c), before
    any credit card issued on the account is activated;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in subparagraph 88(c)(i),
    before the prepaid payment product account is activated;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraph 89(c) and
    subparagraph 89(e)(ii), within 15 days after the day on which the
    trust company becomes the trustee;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraphs 92(c),
    95(4)(b) and 104(c), within 30 days after the day on which the
    information record is created;

  - [(g.1)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraphs 93.1(1)(c)
    and 93.2(1)(c) , at the time the record is created;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 94(c), within
    30 days after the day on which the account is opened;

  - [(h.01)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 95(4)(a),
    before the first transport of cash, virtual currency or negotiable
    instruments is carried out;

  - [(h.1)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 95(4)(a.1),
    at the time the information record is created;

  - [(h.2)]{.lawlabel} in the case referred to in paragraph 95(4)(c), at
    the time of the donation; and

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} in the cases referred to in paragraphs 96(c),
    97(1)(c), 100(c), 101(1)(c), 102(c) and 102.1(c), within 30 days
    after the day on which the transaction is conducted.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If the person or entity that is required to verify an
  entity's identity does so by referring to an electronic version of a
  record that is contained in a database that is accessible to the
  public, they shall keep a record that sets out the entity's
  registration number, the type of record referred to and the source of
  the electronic version of the record. In any other case, they shall
  keep the record or a copy of it.
