### [Record-keeping]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1296589 .Subheading}

**[[144]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-144 .sectionLabel}** A person or entity that
sends a report to the Centre shall keep a copy of the report.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296592){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296592.html"}
:::

**[[145]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-145 .sectionLabel}** Every person and entity
that enters into a business relationship shall keep a record that sets
out the purpose and intended nature of the business relationship.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296595){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296595.html"}
:::

- **[[146]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-146 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person or entity that is required to verify the identity of another
  person or entity shall keep a record of the measures taken when they
  conduct ongoing monitoring of their business relationship with that
  person or entity and of the information obtained from that ongoing
  monitoring.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} to a financial entity in respect of a group plan
    account held within a dividend reinvestment plan or a distribution
    reinvestment plan, including a plan that permits purchases of
    additional shares or units by the member with contributions other
    than the dividends or distributions paid by the sponsor of the plan,
    if the sponsor of the plan is an entity whose shares or units are
    traded on a Canadian stock exchange and that operates in a country
    that is a member of the Financial Action Task Force; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} to a life insurance company or a life insurance
    broker or agent when they are dealing in reinsurance.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296602){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296602.html"}
:::

**[[147]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-147 .sectionLabel}** If a record is required
to be kept under these Regulations, the record or a copy of it may be
kept in a machine-readable or electronic form if a paper copy can
readily be produced from it.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296605){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296605.html"}
:::

- **[[148]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-148 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person or entity that is required to keep records under these
  Regulations shall keep those records for a period of at least five
  years after

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the account to which they relate
    is closed, in the case of signature cards, account operating
    agreements, account applications, credit card applications, records
    setting out the intended use of the account and records that are
    required to be kept under paragraph 12(k) or subsection 123(1);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the day on which the last business transaction is
    conducted, in the case of information records, certificates of
    incorporation, records that are required to be filed annually under
    applicable provincial securities legislation and similar records
    that prove a corporation's existence, partnership agreements,
    articles of association and similar records that prove the existence
    of an entity other than a corporation, records that are required to
    be kept under any of subsections 16(2), 123(2) and (3) and 138(3)
    and (5) and lists and records, other than information records, that
    are required to be kept under section 37; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the day on which they were created, in the case of
    all other records.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, if a record that is kept under
  these Regulations is the property of a person's employer or of a
  person or entity with which the person is in a contractual
  relationship, the person is not required to keep the record after the
  end of their employment or the contractual relationship.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296613){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296613.html"}
:::

**[[149]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-149 .sectionLabel}** Every record that is
required to be kept under these Regulations shall be kept in such a way
that it can be provided to an authorized person within 30 days after the
day on which a request is made to examine it under section 62 of the
Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296616){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296616.html"}
:::

## [PART 6]{.HLabel1}[Exceptions]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1296617 .Part}

### [Payment Card Processing Activities]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1296618 .Subheading}

**[[150]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150 .sectionLabel}** Sections 7, 10 to 14,
16, 85 to 88 and 116, subsections 123(1) and (2), sections 135, 138 and
145 and subsection 148(1) do not apply to the activities of a financial
entity in respect of the processing for a merchant of payments by credit
card or prepaid payment product.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296621){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296621.html"}
:::

### [Electronic Funds Transfer by Credit or Debit Card or Prepaid Payment Product]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1343301 .Subheading}

**[[150.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-150.1 .sectionLabel}** Paragraphs 7(1)(b)
and (c), 12(o) to (q), 13(f) and (g), 14(1)(h) and (i), 70(1)(b) and (c)
and 74(2)(c) to (e), clauses 86(a)(iii)(B) and (F), subparagraphs
116(1)(b)(i) and (ii) and paragraphs 120.2(3)(a) and (b) do not apply in
respect of an electronic funds transfer that is carried out by means of
a credit or debit card or a prepaid payment product if the beneficiary
has an agreement with the payment service provider that permits payment
by that means for the provision of goods and services.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2022-76, s. 9](#1343141-1343304){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1343141-1343304.html"}
:::

### [Requirements with Respect to Virtual Currency]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1296622 .Subheading}

- **[[151]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  greater certainty, paragraphs 7(1)(d), section 11, paragraphs 12(r) to
  (t), 13(h) and 14(1)(j) to (l), sections 19, 21, 26 and 28, paragraphs
  30(1)(f), section 32, paragraph 33(1)(f), section 35, paragraphs
  36(g), (h) and (j), sections 40, 42, 49, 51, 55, 57, 61, 63, 67 and
  69, paragraph 70(1)(d) and sections 73, 79 and 81 do not apply to

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a transfer or receipt of virtual currency as
    compensation for the validation of a transaction that is recorded in
    a distributed ledger; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an exchange, transfer or receipt of a nominal
    amount of virtual currency for the sole purpose of validating
    another transaction or a transfer of information.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} In this section, [[distributed
  ledger]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a digital ledger that is maintained
  by multiple persons or entities and that can only be modified by a
  consensus of those persons or entities.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296629){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296629.html"}
:::

### [Transport of Cash, Virtual Currency or Negotiable Instruments]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1485937 .Subheading}

- **[[151.1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-151.1
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs 4.1(d) and (e) and
  section 37 do not apply in respect of an agreement that applies only
  in respect of the transport of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cash, [[virtual currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
    within the meaning of paragraph (b) of the definition of that term
    in subsection 1(2) or money orders, traveller's cheques or other
    similar negotiable instruments between

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Bank of Canada and a person or entity in
      Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} two financial entities, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} two places of business of the same person or
      entity, if that person or entity is referred to in section 5 of
      the Act and the transport is carried out at their request; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} coins of the currency of Canada for the purposes of
    delivery in accordance with subsection 7(1) of the [Royal Canadian
    Mint Act](/eng/acts/R-9).

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Paragraphs 36(f.1) to (f.3), 95(1)(c.1), (3)(a) and
  (4)(a) and 120(1)(b.1) do not apply in respect of the transport of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} cash, [[virtual currency]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}
    within the meaning of paragraph (b) of the definition of that term
    in subsection 1(2) or money orders, traveller's cheques or other
    similar negotiable instruments between

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the Bank of Canada and a person or entity in
      Canada,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} two financial entities, or

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} two places of business of the same person or
      entity, if that person or entity is referred to in section 5 of
      the Act and the transport is carried out at their request; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} coins of the currency of Canada for the purposes of
    delivery in accordance with subsection 7(1) of the [Royal Canadian
    Mint Act](/eng/acts/R-9).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2023-193, s. 20](#1421180-1485952){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421180-1485952.html"}
:::

### [Requirement To Report Information]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1296630 .Subheading}

- **[[152]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-152 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  requirement to report information set out in any of Schedules 1 to 6
  does not apply in respect of information set out in an item of that
  Schedule that is not marked with an asterisk if, after taking
  reasonable measures to do so, the person or entity is unable to obtain
  the information.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} The requirement to report information set out in any
  of Schedules 1 to 4 and 6 does not apply in respect of information set
  out in an item of that Schedule that is marked with an asterisk if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the information is with respect to two or more cash
    transactions referred to in section 126, electronic funds transfers
    referred to in section 127 or 128, virtual currency transactions
    referred to in section 129 or disbursements referred to in section
    130 that are deemed to be a single transaction of \$10,000 or more;
    and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} after taking reasonable measures to do so, the
    person or entity is unable to obtain the information.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a person or entity is not
  required to report information set out in any item of Schedules 1 to 6
  that is not applicable in the circumstances.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296638){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296638.html"}
:::

### [Requirement To Include Information in a Record]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1296639 .Subheading}

**[[153]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-153 .sectionLabel}** A person or entity that
is required to keep a record under these Regulations is not required to
include information in that record that is readily obtainable from other
records that they are required to keep under these Regulations.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296642){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296642.html"}
:::

### [Other Requirements]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1296643 .Subheading}

- **[[154]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-154
  .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subparagraphs 86(a)(i) and (ii),
  paragraphs 87(a), 89(a) and (d), 94(a), 103(a) and 116(1)(a) and
  subsections 116(2) and (3), 119(1) to (3) and 120.2(1), (2) and (4) do
  not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a business account, if the financial entity,
    securities dealer or casino has already verified the identity of at
    least three persons who are authorized to give instructions in
    respect of the account;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a person who already has an account with the
    financial entity, securities dealer or casino, as the case may be;
    or

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} an account that is opened at the request of an
    entity for the deposit by a life insurance company that is
    affiliated with the entity of a death benefit under a life insurance
    policy or annuity if

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the account is opened in the name of a
      beneficiary that is a person,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} only that death benefit may be deposited into
      the account, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the policy or annuity contract under which the
      claim for the death benefit is made has been in existence for a
      period of at least two years before the day on which the claim is
      made; and

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} an account that is opened for the sale of mutual
    funds if there are reasonable grounds to believe that identity has
    been verified in accordance with subsection 105(1) by a securities
    dealer in respect of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the sale of the mutual funds for which the
      account has been opened, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a transaction that is part of a series of
      transactions that includes that sale.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} Sections 12 to 14, 22, 24.14, 24.2, 29, 43, 45 and
  52, subsection 58(1), sections 64, 74, 82, 86 to 89, 92, 94, 96, 97
  and 100, subsection 101(1) and sections 102 to 104, 116, 117, 119 to
  120.2 and 123 do not apply in respect of

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the sale of an [[exempt
    policy]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef} as defined in subsection 306(1) of
    the [Income Tax Regulations](/eng/regulations/C.R.C.,_c._945);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sale of a group life insurance policy that does
    not provide for a cash surrender value or a savings component;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the sale of an immediate or deferred annuity that
    is paid for entirely with funds that are directly transferred from a
    registered pension plan or from a pension plan that is required to
    be registered under the [Pension Benefits Standards Act,
    1985](/eng/acts/P-7.01), or similar provincial legislation;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the sale of a registered annuity policy or a
    registered retirement income fund;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the sale of an immediate or deferred annuity that
    is paid for entirely with the proceeds of a group life insurance
    policy;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} a transaction that is part of a reverse mortgage or
    structured settlement;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} the opening of an account for the deposit and sale
    of shares from a corporate demutualization or the privatization of a
    Crown corporation;

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} the opening of an account in the name of an
    affiliate of a financial entity, if the affiliate carries out
    activities that are similar to those of persons and entities
    referred to in paragraphs 5(a) to (g) of the Act;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the opening of a registered plan account, including
    a locked-in retirement plan account, a registered retirement savings
    plan account and a group registered retirement savings plan account;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} the opening of an account established in accordance
    with the escrow requirements of a Canadian securities regulator or
    Canadian stock exchange or provincial legislation;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} the opening of an account if the account holder or
    settlor is a pension fund that is regulated under federal or
    provincial legislation;

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} the opening of an account in the name of, or in
    respect of which instructions are authorized to be given by, a
    financial entity, a securities dealer, a life insurance company or
    an investment fund that is regulated under provincial securities
    legislation;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} a public body;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} a corporation or trust that has minimum net assets
    of \$75 million on its last audited balance sheet, whose shares or
    units are traded on a Canadian stock exchange or a stock exchange
    designated under subsection 262(1) of the [Income Tax
    Act](/eng/acts/I-3.3) and that operates in a country that is a
    member of the Financial Action Task Force;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} a subsidiary of a public body referred to in
    paragraph (m) or a corporation or trust referred to in paragraph (n)
    whose financial statements are consolidated with the financial
    statements of that public body, corporation or trust; or

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} the opening of an account solely in the course of
    providing accounting services to a securities dealer.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A financial entity, securities dealer, life insurance
  company or life insurance broker or agent is not required to verify
  the identity of, or to keep a signature card for, a person who is a
  member of a group plan account or to determine whether they are a
  politically exposed foreign person, a politically exposed domestic
  person or a head of an international organization, or a family member
  --- referred to in subsection 2(1) --- of, or a person who is closely
  associated with, one of those persons if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the member's contributions are made by the sponsor
    of the plan or by means of payroll deductions; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the identity of the plan sponsor has been verified
    in accordance with subsection 109(1) or 112(1).

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (3) do not apply if a person or
  entity conducts or attempts to conduct a transaction that is required
  to be reported to the Centre under section 7 of the Act.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296677){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296677.html"}
- [SOR/2020-112, s. 11](#1248075-1296685){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1248075-1296685.html"}
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 16](#1517582-1526895){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517582-1526895.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-154-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 154"){.PITLink}
:::

- **[[155]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-155 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If a
  person or entity verifies a person's identity in accordance with
  subsection 105(1) and complies with section 108 --- or if, before the
  coming into force of this subsection, they ascertained a person's
  identity in accordance with these Regulations, and complied with the
  related record-keeping provisions, as they read at the time --- they
  are not required to verify the person's identity again unless they
  have doubts about the information that was used for that purpose.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If a person or entity verifies a corporation's
  identity in accordance with subsection 109(1) --- or if, before the
  coming into force of this subsection, they confirmed the corporation's
  existence and ascertained its name and address and the names of its
  directors in accordance with these Regulations, and complied with the
  related record-keeping provisions, as they read at the time --- they
  are not required to verify it again unless they have doubts about the
  information that was used for that purpose.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} If a person or entity verifies the identity of an
  entity other than a corporation in accordance with subsection 112(1)
  --- or if, before the coming into force of this subsection, they
  confirmed the entity's existence in accordance with these Regulations,
  and complied with the related record-keeping provisions, as they read
  at the time --- they are not required to verify it again unless they
  have doubts about the information that was used for that purpose.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a person or entity determines that a person is a
  politically exposed foreign person or a family member, referred to in
  subsection 2(1), of such a person --- or if, before the coming into
  force of this subsection, they determined that a person is a
  [[politically exposed foreign person]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}, as
  defined in subsection 9.3(3) of the Act as it read at the time the
  determination was made --- they are not required to make the
  determination again.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296684){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296684.html"}
:::

## [PART 7]{.HLabel1}[Compliance Programs and Special Measures]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1296686 .Part}

- **[[156]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-156 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 9.6(1) of the Act, a person or entity
  referred to in that subsection shall implement the compliance program
  referred to in that subsection by

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} appointing a person who is to be responsible for
    implementing the program or, in the case of a person, taking
    responsibility for implementing the program;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} developing and applying written compliance policies
    and procedures that are kept up to date and, in the case of an
    entity, are approved by a senior officer;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} assessing and documenting the risk referred to in
    subsection 9.6(2) of the Act, taking into consideration

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their clients, business relationships and
      correspondent banking relationships,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} their products, services and delivery channels,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the geographic location of their activities,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity that is referred to in
      any of paragraphs 5(a) to (g) of the Act, any risk resulting from
      the activities of an entity that is affiliated with it and that
      either is referred to in any of those paragraphs or carries out
      activities outside Canada that are similar to those of a person or
      entity referred to in any of those paragraphs, and

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} any other relevant factor;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the person or entity has employees, agents or
    mandataries or other persons who are authorized to act on their
    behalf, developing and maintaining a written, ongoing compliance
    training program for those employees, agents or mandataries or other
    persons;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} instituting and documenting a plan for the ongoing
    compliance training program and delivering the training; and

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} instituting and documenting a plan for a review of
    the compliance program for the purpose of testing its effectiveness.

- [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the person or entity intends to carry out a new
  development or introduce a new technology that may have an impact on
  their clients, business relationships, products, services or delivery
  channels or the geographic location of their activities, they shall,
  in accordance with paragraph (1)(c), assess and document the risk
  referred to in subsection 9.6(2) of the Act before doing so.

- [(3)]{.lawlabel} A review referred to in paragraph (1)(f) shall be
  carried out and the results documented every two years by an internal
  or external auditor of the person or entity, or by the person or
  entity if they do not have an auditor.

- [(4)]{.lawlabel} An entity shall report the findings of the review,
  any updates made to the policies and procedures within the reporting
  period and the status of the implementation of those updates in
  writing to a senior officer within 30 days after the day on which the
  review is completed.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296704){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296704.html"}
- [SOR/2020-112, s. 11](#1248075-1296712){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1248075-1296712.html"}
- [SOR/2023-194, s. 15](#1421299-1492515){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1421299-1492515.html"}
:::

::: PITLink
[Previous
Version](section-156-20210601.html "Link to previous version of section 156"){.PITLink}
:::

**[[157]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-157 .sectionLabel}** The prescribed special
measures that are required to be taken by a person or entity referred to
in subsection 9.6(1) of the Act for the purposes of subsection 9.6(3) of
the Act are the development and application of written policies and
procedures for

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} taking enhanced measures, based on an assessment of
  the risk, to verify the identity of any person or entity; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} taking any other enhanced measure to mitigate the
  risks, including

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} ensuring, at a frequency appropriate to the level
    of risk, that client identification information and information
    collected under section 138 is up to date, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} conducting, at a frequency appropriate to the
    level of risk, the ongoing monitoring of business relationships
    referred to in section 123.1.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2019-240, s. 46](#1179483-1296711){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1179483-1296711.html"}
- [SOR/2020-112, s. 11](#1248075-1296713){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1248075-1296713.html"}
:::

## [PART 8]{.HLabel1}[Disclosure, Collection and Use of Personal Information]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1522763 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definition of
[[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinitionRef}

**[[158]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-158 .sectionLabel}** In this Part,
[[Commissioner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the Privacy Commissioner
appointed under section 53 of the [Privacy Act](/eng/acts/P-21).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 17](#1517584-1522767){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517584-1522767.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Code of practice

**[[159]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-159 .sectionLabel}** For the purposes of
section 11.01 of the Act, a person or entity referred to in that section
shall

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} establish and implement a code of practice for the
  disclosure, collection and use of personal information that has been
  approved by the Commissioner under section 163; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} ensure that the disclosure, collection and use of
  personal information is carried out in accordance with the approved
  code of practice.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 17](#1517584-1522773){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517584-1522773.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirements

**[[160]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-160 .sectionLabel}** A code of practice
shall meet the following requirements:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} it identifies the persons or entities referred to in
  section 5 of the Act that are subject to the code;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} it describes the personal information of an
  individual that may be disclosed, collected or used without their
  knowledge or consent;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} it describes the purposes for which an individual's
  personal information may be disclosed, collected or used without their
  knowledge or consent;

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} it describes the manner in which an individual's
  personal information may be disclosed, collected or used without their
  knowledge or consent;

- [(e)]{.lawlabel} it describes the measures to be taken to ensure the
  protection of personal information, including measures concerning the
  retention of such information and the keeping of records; and

- [(f)]{.lawlabel} it includes information demonstrating that the code
  complies with the requirements of the Act and provides for
  substantially the same or greater protection of personal information
  as that provided under the [Personal Information Protection and
  Electronic Documents Act](/eng/acts/P-8.6).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 17](#1517584-1522783){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517584-1522783.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Application for approval

- **[[161]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-161 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  person or entity referred to in section 5 of the Act, or any person or
  entity acting on behalf of persons or entities referred to in that
  section, may apply to the Commissioner for approval of a code of
  practice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Acknowledgement

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the application for approval is made by or on
  behalf of two or more persons or entities, it shall include an
  acknowledgement that each of those persons or entities has approved
  the code and has consented to its submission to the Commissioner.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Additional information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the information provided by the applicant is
  insufficient to permit the Commissioner to decide whether the code of
  practice meets the requirements set out in section 160, the
  Commissioner may request from the applicant any additional information
  necessary to make that decision and may suspend the processing of the
  application until that information is provided.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Code provided to Centre

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The applicant shall, no later than the day on which
  the application for approval is made, notify the Centre of the
  application and provide it with a copy of the code of practice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Comments from Centre

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} The Centre may provide comments on the code of
  practice to the applicant or the Commissioner, or to both.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit for comments

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner shall consider the comments provided
  by the Centre in making a decision with respect to the code of
  practice, unless the comments are provided more than 60 days after the
  day on which the application for approval is made.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 17](#1517584-1522798){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517584-1522798.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Time limit for decision

- **[[162]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-162 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commissioner shall notify the applicant of their decision no later
  than 120 days after the day on which an application for approval of a
  code of practice is made under section 161 and, if the application is
  refused, shall provide their reasons in writing.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Extension of time limit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner may extend the period referred to in
  subsection (1) by no more than 15 days and, in that case, the
  Commissioner shall notify the applicant of the extension.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Excluded period

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The calculation of the periods referred to in
  subsections (1) and (2) shall exclude any period during which the
  processing of the application is suspended by the Commissioner under
  subsection 161(3).

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 17](#1517584-1522807){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517584-1522807.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Approval

- **[[163]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-163 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Commissioner shall approve the code of practice if the Commissioner is
  satisfied that it meets the requirements set out in section 160.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed approval

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the Commissioner does not notify the applicant of
  their decision before the end of the period referred to in subsection
  162(1) or, in the case of an extension, the extended period referred
  to in subsection 162(2), the code of practice is deemed to be approved
  as of the end of that period.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 17](#1517584-1522819){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517584-1522819.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice to Centre

**[[164]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-164 .sectionLabel}** The Commissioner shall
notify the Centre of any decision to approve a code of practice or
refuse an application for approval and of any deemed approval of a code.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 17](#1517584-1522823){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517584-1522823.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revision to code

- **[[165]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-165 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person or entity referred to in section 5 of the Act that makes any
  revision to an approved code of practice shall, as soon as feasible,
  notify the Commissioner and the Centre of the revision and provide
  them with a copy of the revised code of practice.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Revision considered significant

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The Commissioner may, no later than 30 days after
  receiving notice of a revision to a code of practice, notify the
  person or entity that the Commissioner considers the revision to be
  significant and direct the person or entity to apply for approval of
  the revised code under section 161.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No revision notice

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If the Commissioner has reasonable grounds to believe
  that a person or entity has revised an approved code of practice but
  has failed to notify the Commissioner, the Commissioner may direct the
  person or entity to apply for approval of the revised code under
  section 161.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Suspension

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a person or entity fails to comply with the
  Commissioner's direction, the Commissioner may suspend the approval
  previously given under section 163 with respect to the code of
  practice.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 17](#1517584-1522834){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517584-1522834.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Code of practice in effect

**[[166]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-166 .sectionLabel}** A person or entity that
has revised an approved code of practice shall continue to follow the
code as it read when it was previously approved by the Commissioner
until

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Commissioner notifies the person or entity that
  the revised code meets the requirements set out in section 160, if the
  person or entity has been directed by the Commissioner to apply for
  approval of the revised code; or

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the 30-day period referred to in subsection 165(2)
  ends, if the Commissioner has not notified the person or entity that
  the revision is considered to be significant.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 17](#1517584-1522840){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517584-1522840.html"}
:::

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Renewal of approval every five years

**[[167]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-167 .sectionLabel}** A person or entity
referred to in section 5 of the Act with respect to whom a code of
practice has been approved shall, every five years after the day of the
most recent approval of the code, reapply for approval of the code under
section 161.

::: HistoricalNote
- [SOR/2025-68, s. 17](#1517584-1522844){.wb-lbx
  ajax-after="1517584-1522844.html"}
:::

## RELATED PROVISIONS {#related-provisions .scheduleTitleText}

- --- SOR/2016-153, s. 124, as amended by SOR/2017-115, s. 1

  - [124]{.lawlabel} A person or entity may, during the period that
    begins on the day on which section 70 of these Regulations comes
    into force and ends on January 23, 2018, comply with subsection
    64(1) or (1.1) and sections 64.1 and 67 of the [Proceeds of Crime
    (Money Laundering) and Terrorist Financing
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2002-184), as they read
    immediately before the day on which that section 70 comes into
    force.
:::

:::: section
::: ScheduleNIF
## AMENDMENTS NOT IN FORCE {#amendments-not-in-force .scheduleTitleText}

- --- SOR/2024-266, s. 1

  - [1]{.lawlabel} The [Proceeds of Crime (Money Laundering) and
    Terrorist Financing
    Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2002-184)[[Footnote
    ]{.wb-invisible}^1^](#fn_81000-2-5459_hq_1711117456){#fn_81000-2-5459_hq_1711117456-ID0EBCATAA}
    are amended by adding the following after section 37:

    :::: section

    - - [37.1]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The documents referred to in

        months before the day on which they are reviewed under
        subsection 9.93(1) of the Act.

      - [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 9.93(4) of the
        Act, a money services business must retain the documents that it
        obtains under subsection 9.93(1) of the Act for a period of five
        years after the day on which the records are obtained.

- --- SOR/2024-267, s. 9

  - [9]{.lawlabel} Section 36 of the Regulations is amended by striking
    out "and" at the end of paragraph (i), by adding "and" at the end of
    paragraph (j) and by adding the following after paragraph (j):

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - [(k)]{.lawlabel} if they provide acquirer services in respect of a
      private automated banking machine, a record of

      - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, email address and telephone
        number of the owner, the lessor and the operator of the private
        automated banking machine, the nature of their principal
        business or their occupation and, in the case of a person, their
        date of birth,

      - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name, address, email address and telephone
        number of the owner of the cash that is loaded into the private
        automated banking machine, as well as

        - [(A)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a person, their date of birth
          and the nature of their principal business or their
          occupation, and

        - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity, its registration or
          incorporation number, the jurisdiction and country of issue of
          that number and the nature of its principal business,

      - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the number of the settlement account for the
        private automated banking machine and the name and address of
        each account holder, as well as

        - [(A)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a person, their date of birth
          and the nature of their principal business or their
          occupation, and

        - [(B)]{.lawlabel} in the case of an entity, its registration or
          incorporation number, the jurisdiction and country of issue of
          that number and the nature of its principal business,

      - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} the terminal identification number of the
        private automated banking machine,

      - [(v)]{.lawlabel} the brand, model and serial number of the
        private automated banking machine,

      - [(vi)]{.lawlabel} the number of bills that the private automated
        banking machine can contain,

      - [(vii)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the place of business
        at which the private automated banking machine is located and
        the nature of the principal business of the place of business,

      - [(viii)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the business relationship
        between the owner of the cash, the owner, the lessor and the
        operator of the private automated banking machine and the owner
        of the place of business at which the machine is located,

      - [(ix)]{.lawlabel} the source of the cash that is loaded into the
        private automated banking machine, and

      - [(x)]{.lawlabel} the method used to transport the cash.
    :::
    ::::

- --- SOR/2024-267, s. 11

  - [11]{.lawlabel} The Regulations are amended by adding the following
    after section 64.6:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    #### [Title Insurers]{.HTitleText2} {#title-insurers .SchedHeadL2}

    - [64.7]{.lawlabel} A title insurer is engaged in a business or
      profession for the purposes of paragraph 5(j) of the Act when they
      provide a title insurance policy to the purchaser of real property
      or an immovable.

    - [64.8]{.lawlabel} A title insurer must keep, in respect of every
      title insurance policy that they provide to the purchaser of real
      property or an immovable, a record of

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the name and address of the purchaser and, in
        the case of a person, their date of birth;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the legal description and address of the real
        property or immovable;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the closing date of the purchase;

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the purchase price;

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} the amount of any loan secured by a mortgage on
        the real property or a hypothec on the immovable and the name of
        the lender;

      - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if known, the name of the vendor; and

      - [(g)]{.lawlabel} any title information respecting the real
        property or immovable that is found in the land registry in
        which the title to the real property or immovable is recorded.
    :::
    ::::

- --- SOR/2024-267, s. 14

  - [14]{.lawlabel} The Regulations are amended by adding the following
    after section 102.1:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    #### [Title Insurers]{.HTitleText2} {#title-insurers-1 .SchedHeadL2}

    - [102.2]{.lawlabel} A title insurer must

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 105, verify the
        identity of a person who is the purchaser of real property or an
        immovable and to whom they provide a title insurance policy;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 109, verify the
        identity of a corporation that is the purchaser of real property
        or an immovable and to which they provide a title insurance
        policy; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in accordance with section 112, verify the
        identity of an entity, other than a corporation, that is the
        purchaser of real property or an immovable and to which they
        provide a title insurance policy.
    :::
    ::::

- --- SOR/2024-267, s. 17

  - [17]{.lawlabel} The Regulations are amended by adding the following
    after section 109:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - - [109.1]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person or entity that is
        required to verify a corporation's identity in accordance with
        subsection 109(1) may rely on an agent or mandatary to take the
        measures to do so.

      - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person or entity may rely on measures that
        were previously taken by an agent or mandatary to verify the
        corporation's identity if the agent or mandatary was, at the
        time they took the measures,

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acting in their own capacity, whether or not
          they were required to take the measures under these
          Regulations; or

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acting as an agent or mandatary under a
          written agreement or arrangement that was entered into, with
          another person or entity that is required to verify a
          corporation's identity, for the purposes of verifying identity
          in accordance with subsection 109(1).

      - [(3)]{.lawlabel} In order to rely, under subsection (1) or (2),
        on measures taken by an agent or mandatary, the person or entity
        must

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have entered into a written agreement or
          arrangement with the agent or mandatary for the purposes of
          verifying a corporation's identity in accordance with
          subsection 109(1);

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as soon as feasible, obtain from the agent or
          mandatary the information that the agent or mandatary referred
          to in order to verify the corporation's identity and the
          information that the agent or mandatary confirmed as being
          that of the corporation; and

        - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be satisfied that the information that the
          agent or mandatary confirmed as being that of the corporation
          is valid and current and that the agent or mandatary verified
          the corporation's identity in the manner described in
          subsection 109(1).
    :::
    ::::

- --- SOR/2024-267, s. 19

  - [19]{.lawlabel} The Regulations are amended by adding the following
    after section 112:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - - [112.1]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} A person or entity that is
        required to verify an entity's identity in accordance with
        subsection 112(1) may rely on an agent or mandatary to take the
        measures to do so.

      - [(2)]{.lawlabel} The person or entity may rely on measures that
        were previously taken by an agent or mandatary to verify the
        entity's identity if the agent or mandatary was, at the time
        they took the measures,

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} acting in their own capacity, whether or not
          they were required to take the measures under these
          Regulations; or

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} acting as an agent or mandatary under a
          written agreement or arrangement that was entered into, with
          another person or entity that is required to verify an
          entity's identity, for the purposes of verifying identity in
          accordance with subsection 112(1).

      - [(3)]{.lawlabel} In order to rely, under subsection (1) or (2),
        on measures taken by an agent or mandatary, the person or entity
        must

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} have entered into a written agreement or
          arrangement with the agent or mandatary for the purposes of
          verifying an entity's identity in accordance with subsection
          112(1);

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} as soon as feasible, obtain from the agent or
          mandatary the information that the agent or mandatary referred
          to in order to verify the entity's identity and the
          information that the agent or mandatary confirmed as being
          that of the entity; and

        - [(c)]{.lawlabel} be satisfied that the information that the
          agent or mandatary confirmed as being that of the entity is
          valid and current and that the agent or mandatary verified the
          entity's identity in the manner described in subsection
          112(1).
    :::
    ::::

- --- SOR/2025-68, s. 13

  - [13]{.lawlabel} Subsection 138(2) of the Regulations is replaced by
    the following:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Every person or entity that is subject to
      subsection (1) shall take reasonable measures to confirm the
      accuracy of the information when it is first obtained under that
      subsection and in the course of ongoing monitoring of business
      relationships. In the case of information that is related to a
      corporation incorporated under the [Canada Business Corporations
      Act](/eng/acts/C-44), the person or entity shall consult
      information that is made available to the public under section
      21.303 of that Act if they consider, based on a risk assessment
      referred to in subsection 9.6(2) of the Act, that there is a high
      risk of a money laundering offence or terrorist activity financing
      offence.
    :::
    ::::

- --- SOR/2025-68, s. 14

  - [14]{.lawlabel} The Regulations are amended by adding the following
    after section 138:

    :::: section
    ::: AmendedText
    - - [138.1]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Every person or entity that
        identifies a material discrepancy between the information that
        they obtain under paragraphs 138(1)(a) and (d) and the
        information that they consult in accordance with subsection
        138(2) shall

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} report the discrepancy to the Director
          appointed under section 260 of the [Canada Business
          Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-44) within 30 days after the day
          on which the discrepancy is identified, together with the
          information set out in Schedule 7; and

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} keep a copy of any acknowledgement of receipt
          of the report.

      - [(2)]{.lawlabel} A person or entity is not required to report
        under paragraph (1)(a) if the material discrepancy is resolved
        within 30 days after the day on which it is identified.

      - [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of this section, a material
        discrepancy does not include a discrepancy that arises from

        - [(a)]{.lawlabel} an error in spelling or a minor variation in
          a name or address;

        - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the use of a service address in one
          information source and a residential address in another;

        - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the fact that information is not available to
          the public due to an exception or exemption under subsection
          21.303(2) or (3) of the [Canada Business Corporations
          Act](/eng/acts/C-44); or

        - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the fact that information that is made
          available to the public under section 21.303 of the [Canada
          Business Corporations Act](/eng/acts/C-44) is about persons
          who are not referred to in paragraph 138(1)(a) of these
          Regulations, or vice versa.
    :::
    ::::

- --- SOR/2025-68, s. 15

  - - [15]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 152(1) of the

      :::: section

      - - [152]{.lawlabel} [(1)]{.lawlabel} The requirement to report
          information set out in any of Schedules 1 to 7 does not apply
          in respect of information set out in an item of that Schedule
          that is not marked with an asterisk if, after taking
          reasonable measures to do so, the person or entity is unable
          to obtain the information.

    - [(2)]{.lawlabel} Subsection 152(3) of the Regulations is replaced
      by the following:

      :::: section
      ::: AmendedText
      - [(3)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, a person or entity is
        not required to report information set out in any item of
        Schedules 1 to 7 that is not applicable in the circumstances.
      :::
      ::::
